WORLDS achieves 51.8% navigation success across all 5,311 CityNav test episodes, the highest reported success rate, exceeding the previous published best by 15.7 percentage points under an OSM-only, high-resolution orthographic protocol. On 1,000 shared episodes, it achieves 50.0% versus 27.9% for the strongest adapted baseline using the same model, prior, sensing stack, and movement budget. Observation-based verification by the Examiner contributes 5.9 points of this success, and at a reduced reasoning-effort setting WORLDS still exceeds the adapted GeoNav baseline by 18.8 points while generating fewer tokens. We also demonstrate WORLDS on a quadrotor, which flies the generated sensing waypoints and grounds three language targets, including a vehicle absent from the map, from its onboard imagery.

</details>


### [106] [Object-Centered Reconstruction for Vision-Based 3D Force Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23856)
*Zhonghao Zhang,Mingyeung Wu,Hao Yang,Ayberk Acar,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉的3D交互力估计流水线，通过立体内窥镜视频中软组织形变估计力，在体外与离体实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人结直肠手术中过大的力会损伤组织并增加吻合口漏风险。达芬奇5具备力感知功能，但早期达芬奇系统及其他手术机器人平台尚无此能力，因此需要一种无需力传感器的力估计方法。

Method: 动态重建以物体为中心的坐标系下的组织点云，采用几何约束跟踪组织点，并用神经网络预测3D力向量；在橡胶手套模型、离体猪结肠及在体结直肠手术视频序列上逐步评估。

Result: 在模型和猪结肠上平均RMSE分别为0.77 N和1.30 N；相比相机坐标系表示，物体中心表示使RMSE降低51.3%和56.7%；相比CoTracker，几何约束跟踪使RMSE降低19.8%和25.3%；并在在体结直肠手术序列上定性验证可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了基于视觉、无力传感器力估计在临床转化方向上的可行性，可在缺乏力感知的机器人平台上实现软组织交互力估计。

Abstract: Excessive force may damage tissue and increase the risk of anastomotic leakage in robotic colorectal surgery. Although the da Vinci 5 provides force sensing, this capability is unavailable on earlier da Vinci systems and many other surgical robotic platforms. In this work, we present a vision-based pipeline for estimating 3D interaction forces from soft-tissue deformation in stereo endoscopic video. We dynamically reconstruct the tissue point cloud in an object-centered coordinate frame, track tissue points with geometric constraints, and predict the 3D force vector with a neural network. We progressively evaluate the pipeline on rubber-glove phantoms, ex vivo porcine colons, and in vivo colorectal surgical video sequences. Under varying tissue orientations and positions within the endoscopic view, as well as different camera viewpoints, the proposed method achieves average root mean square error (RMSEs) of 0.77 N and 1.30 N on the phantom and porcine colon, respectively. Compared with the camera-frame representation, the object-centered representation reduces average RMSE by 51.3% and 56.7%, while geometry-constrained tracking reduces RMSE by 19.8% and 25.3% compared with CoTracker. We further qualitatively demonstrate the feasibility of vision-based force estimation on an in vivo colorectal surgical sequence, as a step toward clinical translation of vision-based, sensorless force estimation.

</details>


### [107] [Grounded Action Model: 3D Grounding as a Foundation for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23863)
*Gehao Zhang,Weikai Huang,Shailesh Shailesh,Yiyan Peng,Jiafei Duan,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Grounded Action Models (GAM)，一种以3D grounding构建的机器人基础模型新范式，通过语言、点、框等提示转为对象为中心的共享表示，实现动作预测与长程操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型（VLA、WAM）的预训练骨干不直接要求度量化的grounding，导致"哪些物体重要、在哪里"只能从机器人示范中隐式学习，缺乏显式的对象级几何与度量支撑。

Method: GAM使用语言、点或框提示，先转换为选定对象的共享对象为中心表示，包含目标聚焦的视觉特征与度量物体几何；该表示与机器人状态历史经多流transformer混合以预测动作块。可自主运行，也可作为低级控制器受高层规划器控制，支持长程与依赖记忆的操作。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0上50个任务平均成功率55.3%（对比Spatial Forcing 52.0%），场景随机化下47.6%（对比Abot-M0 30.4%），且动作策略仅在干净场景示范上训练；LIBERO-PRO上16种扰动设置SOTA平均61%（对比π0.5 53%），目标重定位或新指定时增益最大；双机械臂YAM上视觉偏移下17/20成功（π0.5为4/20），与Molmo2规划器组合在Franka上长程与记忆任务达64.7% ID和49.8% OOD步骤完成率。

Conclusion: 显式的3D对象级grounding可显著提升机器人基础模型在场景随机化、目标扰动与视觉偏移下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，并可作为高层规划器的通用低级控制器。

Abstract: Manipulation policies must know which objects matter and where they are, yet the pretrained backbones that current robot foundation models build on, from language in vision-language-action models (VLAs) to video generation in world-action models (WAMs), do not directly require this metric grounding, leaving it to be learned implicitly from robot demonstrations. We propose Grounded Action Models (GAMs), a new paradigm of robot foundation models built with 3D grounding. GAM can be conditioned using language, points, or box prompts, which are first transformed into a shared object-centric representation of the selected objects. This representation captures target-focused visual features and metric object geometry, which is mixed with robot state history through a multi-stream transformer to predict action chunks. Although GAMs can be run autonomously, they can also serve as a low-level controller that a high-level planner controls using its various input modalities, allowing for long-horizon and memory-dependent manipulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, GAM achieves an average success rate of 55.3% across 50 tasks (vs. 52.0% for Spatial Forcing), including 47.6% under scene randomization (vs. 30.4% for Abot-M0), with its action policy trained only on clean-scene demonstrations. On LIBERO-PRO, it achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 61% (vs. 53% for $π_{0.5}$) across 16 perturbation settings, with the largest gains when targets are relocated or newly designated. On two real robots, GAM retains 17/20 successes under visual shift on a bimanual YAM versus 4/20 for $π_{0.5}$, while its composition with a Molmo2 planner on a Franka achieves 64.7% ID and 49.8% OOD step completion on long-horizon and memory-dependent tasks.

</details>


### [108] [HumynexSurg-1: A Curated Expert Liposuction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23885)
*Rhea Huang,David L. Matlock,Laurence Reich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 发布首个抽脂手术机器人专家数据集 HumynexSurg-1：14 段猪腹部组织示范、42,738 帧、35.6 分钟，含同步吸力、六轴手部力/力矩、RGB-D 与侧视视频及语音标注，并验证 NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 可在一小时内完成微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型的示范语料缺少手术数据：780 小时 Open-H 数据集中仅一个含同步力信息，且无美学类手术。抽脂尤为困难——器械在皮下工作，医生依赖触感与判断。因此需要为这类手术构建高质量专家数据集。

Method: 由资深抽脂医生在猪腹部组织上操作并全程口述决策，采集同步吸力压力、六轴手部力/力矩、俯视 RGB-D、侧视视频和领夹麦克风音频；采用专利待审的感知方案，围绕策略所需物理量组织通道，使当前由模型捕获的通道日后可升级为传感器而不改变数据格式。当前版本以侧视视频工具-手轨迹记录器械运动，力通道作为状态；后续采集将加入实测 6-DoF 手柄位姿、经验证的力通道、脂肪层超声成像与触诊传感。

Result: 共 14 个回合、42,738 帧、35.6 分钟、356 条语音，其中 95% 可编译为抽脂专用标签模式。NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 无需自定义代码即可微调，每轮不到一小时并学会所录操作；扩展探针显示每新增一个 session 都能降低未见 session 上的误差。

Conclusion: 数据集、标签模式、质量保证报告与评测协议本身即为产品；探针结果表明下一步应采集多个跨脂肪区域的短 session，并配备上述传感器。

Abstract: Robot foundation models learn manipulation from large demonstration corpora, but surgery is missing from those corpora: across the 780-hour Open-H surgical collection, one dataset carries synchronized force and none covers an aesthetic procedure. Liposuction is the hard case, because the instrument works under the skin and the surgeon operates by feel and by judgment. Humynex Robotics builds curated expert datasets for this kind of procedure. HumynexSurg-1 is the first release: a master liposuction surgeon performing on porcine abdominal tissue while narrating every decision, recorded with synchronized suction pressure, six-axis hand force/torque, top-down RGB-D video, side video and a lavalier microphone -- 14 episodes, 42,738 frames, 35.6 minutes, 356 utterances of which 95% compile into a liposuction-specific label schema. The capture follows a patent-pending sensing plan organized around the quantities a policy needs, so a channel captured today by a model can be upgraded to a sensor tomorrow without changing the data format. This release captures the instrument motion as a tool-hand track in the side video and provides the force channel as state; the funded capture adds a measured 6-DoF handle pose, a validated force channel, ultrasound imaging of the fat layer, and palpation sensing. As a proof of concept, NVIDIA Isaac GR00T N1.7 fine-tunes on the dataset with no custom code in under an hour per run and learns the recorded sessions; scaling probes on the same episodes show where further gains come from: every new session lowers the error on an unseen session. The dataset, its label schema, its quality-assurance reports and its evaluation protocol are the product; the next capture, many short sessions across fat regions with the sensors named here, is what the probes point to.

</details>


### [109] [HapticWAM: Distilling Imagined Touch into a World-Action Model without Inference-Time Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23888)
*Mikhail Sannikov,Ilya Mikhalchuk,Konstantin Gubernatorov,Petr Kovalev,Ogunwoye Faith Oluwatobi,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticWAM是一种世界-动作模型，通过异构触觉编码、结构化接触预测与师生蒸馏，实现部署时无需指尖观测的富接触操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作需要估计力、滑动和接触几何，而这些在场景图像中往往模糊不清；光学触觉传感器虽提供视觉与力学信号，但从这些信号中学习面临两大挑战：如何超越外观表示接触，以及如何将其优势迁移到部署时不需要指尖观测的策略上。

Method: 提出HapticWAM：教师模型将凝胶图像与形变、剪切、分布力、合成力矩及派生接触状态编码进冻结的视频主干；模型联合生成动作与描述未来事件和力学的"接触包"；使用"预期接触耦合"以先前想象的接触包调节注意力，在缺少直接触觉观测时保留接触相关输入；通过"触觉-想象蒸馏"将接触未来与动作预测迁移到学生模型，学生保留生成式接触头但移除指尖输入分支。

Result: 在真实环境中三个富接触抓放任务上，HapticWAM学生模型达到77%的每任务平均成功率（50次起步中41次成功，合并成功率82%），其中一个任务达95%，优于所评估的教师模型和基线配置。

Conclusion: 结合异构触觉编码、结构化接触预测与师生蒸馏，可在部署时无需指尖触觉观测的情况下，有效提升富接触操作策略的性能。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires estimating forces, slip and contact geometry that can remain ambiguous in scene images. Optical tactile sensors provide both visual observations of the contact surface and mechanical measurements, yet learning from these signals raises two challenges: representing contact beyond appearance and transferring its benefits to a policy that does not require fingertip observations at deployment. We introduce HapticWAM, a world-action model that combines heterogeneous tactile encoding, structured contact prediction and teacher-student distillation. Its teacher encodes gel images together with deformation, shear, distributed forces, resultant wrench and derived contact state into a frozen video backbone. Rather than predicting tactile pixels alone, the model jointly generates actions and a contact package describing future events and mechanics. Anticipatory Contact Coupling uses the previously imagined package to condition attention, preserving a contact-related input when direct tactile observations are unavailable. Haptic-Imagination Distillation transfers both contact futures and action predictions to a student that retains the generative contact head but removes its fingertip input branches. On a real-world setup, across three contact-rich pick-and-place tasks, HapticWAM Student achieves a 77% per-task mean success rate (41 of 50 starts, 82% pooled), reaching 95% on one of the tasks, outperforming the evaluated teacher and baseline configurations.

</details>


### [110] [BarrierFormer: Transformer-Guided Predictive Barrier Enforcement for Safe Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23896)
*Anandsingh Chauhan,Kunal Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BarrierFormer：一种基于障碍函数监督的Transformer框架，通过在学习无模型安全策略时编码 rollout 级CBF约束，实现实时、无模型、预测性的安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CBF仅保证当前时刻安全，具有短视性，系统可能被推向未来无可行安全控制的边界；MPC方法虽在预测时域上施加约束，但需已知模型并在每步求解约束优化，计算代价高，难以实时部署。

Method: 提出BarrierFormer：因果Transformer编码观测-动作历史，通过动力学头自回归生成预测rollout以替代模型，通过动作头为标称控制器提供残差修正以替代在线计算；障碍评论家基于局部观测评估rollout上的CBF约束违反，安全教师计算满足约束的障碍一致动作作为直接监督目标。

Result: 在线性和非线性、2D和3D动力系统的安全目标导航任务上，BarrierFormer在安全率和推理延迟方面优于现有RL、扩散、MPC和Transformer方法。

Conclusion: BarrierFormer在推理时仅将观测-动作历史映射为控制动作，无需在线优化或模型知识，实现了实时无模型预测性安全约束。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) have become one of the most popular tools for encoding and enforcing state constraints in safety-critical robotics. Standard CBF approaches are inherently myopic in nature as they enforce safety only at the current time step. Consequently, the system can be driven toward the boundary of the safe set where no feasible safe control exists at a future timestep. Model predictive control (MPC) based approaches address this by enforcing state constraints over a receding horizon. However, such approaches generally require the model to be known for solving a constrained optimization problem at every step, which is computationally expensive for real-time deployment. We propose BarrierFormer, a barrier-supervised transformer framework that addresses these limitations by encoding rollout-level CBF constraints in learning a model-free safe policy. A causal transformer encodes observation-action history, autoregressively generates a predictive rollout through the dynamics head to replace the model, and provides a residual correction to a nominal controller through the action head to replace the online computation. A barrier critic operating on local observations evaluates CBF constraint violations along this rollout, and a safety teacher computes barrier-consistent actions satisfying these constraints as direct supervision targets for the learned control policy. During inference, the policy maps observation-action history to control actions without any online optimization or model knowledge, enabling real-time model-free predictive safety enforcement. Evaluations across linear and nonlinear, 2D and 3D dynamical systems for safe goal-directed navigation demonstrate that BarrierFormer outperforms existing reinforcement learning (RL)-based, diffusion-based, MPC-based, and transformer-based approaches in safety rate and inference latency.

</details>


### [111] [ReVeal: A Reconstruction-Aware Real-to-Sim Framework for VLA Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23910)
*Xinyi Wang,Heng Hao,Wenjun Hu,Anna Enyu Li,Dizhi Ma,Karthik Ramani,Hankyu Moon,Yeong-Dae Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReVeal框架，用于真实到仿真环境的评估，结合重建、保真度指标与匹配闭环策略评估，验证重建保真度与真实-仿真性能一致性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的VLA策略评估可扩展且可复现，但重建误差会导致仿真性能与真实性能偏离，因此需要评估重建环境对下游VLA策略评估的适用性。

Method: ReVeal框架包含工作空间重建、重建级别评估和匹配闭环策略评估；提出NVMF（观察保真度）与APGF（平面几何保真度）两项指标；并开发PGSR-D重建流程，引入单目深度监督以在多视角视觉线索有限时改善几何。

Result: 在8个评估场景中，NVMF与APGF能一致区分2DGS、PGSR和PGSR-D的保真度；对GR00T、SmolVLA和pi0.5在8个人形操作任务上的匹配评估显示，重建保真度与真实-仿真性能一致性在不同流程间呈现稳定排序。

Conclusion: 进一步分析表明，更高的重建保真度与更强的真实-仿真一致性相关，说明保真度指标可用于评估仿真环境对VLA策略评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Simulation-based evaluation provides a scalable and repeatable alternative to real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies. However, reconstruction errors can cause simulated policy performance to diverge from real-world performance, motivating the need to assess reconstructed environments for downstream VLA policy evaluation. We present ReVeal, a real-to-sim assessment framework combining workspace reconstruction, reconstruction-level assessment, and matched closed-loop policy evaluation. Novel-View Mesh Fidelity (NVMF) and Annotated Planar Geometry Fidelity (APGF) assess observation and planar geometric fidelity, respectively. We also develop PGSR-D, a reconstruction pipeline incorporating monocular depth supervision to improve geometry where multi-view visual cues are limited. Across 8 assessment scenes, NVMF and APGF consistently distinguish the fidelity of 2DGS, PGSR, and PGSR-D. Matched evaluations of GR00T, SmolVLA, and pi0.5 across 8 humanoid manipulation tasks show consistent ordering between reconstruction fidelity and real-sim performance agreement across pipelines. Further analysis of the evaluation workspaces shows that higher fidelity is associated with stronger real-sim agreement.

</details>


### [112] [MR-SPITE: Accelerating Multi-Robot Conflict Scans via Hierarchical Swept-Volume Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23928)
*Marta Markowicz,James Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR-SPITE 是一种基于运动分段的保守过滤器，用于加速多机器人路径间的冲突扫描，通过时间区间调度和保守边界比较来认证无冲突窗口，仅将未解决窗口交给底层碰撞检查器，在 16 个 Fetch 机器人上实现 7.18 倍冲突扫描加速和 57% 规划时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人同步路径的冲突扫描需要详细的碰撞检查，可能涉及每个时间步上的每一对机器人，并且随着冲突被修复可能需要重复多次，计算开销巨大。因此需要一种能高效过滤冲突、减少底层碰撞检查调用次数的方法。

Method: 提出 MR-SPITE（Multi-Robot SPITE），一种保守的、基于运动分段的过滤器。它将每条路径划分为时间区间，为每个分段分配保守边界；通过区间调度器比较时间上重叠运动的边界：若边界不相交则认证该共享窗口无冲突，若无法解决则交给底层碰撞检查器。方法被集成到 ARC 中，并与基于 VAMP 的碰撞检查结合。

Result: 对于 16 个 Fetch 机器人，集成 MR-SPITE 的 ARC 相比使用 PRM+VAMP 的基线 ARC 实现，冲突扫描配对中位加速达 7.18 倍，规划时间中位数降低 57%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，运动分段边界能够与配置级碰撞加速互补，同时在保持底层离散扫描器行为的前提下显著提升多机器人冲突扫描与规划效率。

Abstract: Conflict scanning over synchronized robot paths requires detailed collision checking, potentially across every robot pair at every timestep, and may be repeated many times as conflicts are repaired. We present Multi-Robot SPITE (MR-SPITE), a conservative, motion-segment-based filter for accelerating these scans. MR-SPITE partitions each path into temporal intervals and assigns conservative bounds to each segment. An interval scheduler compares bounds for temporally overlapping motions: disjoint bounds certify the shared window as conflict-free, while unresolved windows are passed to the underlying collision checker. We integrate MR-SPITE into ARC and combine it with VAMP-based collision checking. For 16 Fetch robots, ARC with MR-SPITE achieves a paired median conflict scan speedup of 7.18x and reduces median planning time by 57% relative to the baseline ARC implementation with PRM+VAMP. These results demonstrate that motion-segment bounds complement configuration-level collision acceleration while preserving the behavior of the underlying discretized scanner.

</details>


### [113] [FinsSim: A Reality-Aligned Integrated Simulation Platform for Underwater Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23943)
*Yu Zhang,Yuanmingqing Song,Xiangyun Rao,Pangkit Fong,Kunhao Zhang,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FinsSim 是一个面向水下机器人 Sim-to-Real 学习的逼真对齐集成仿真平台，集成高保真仿真、标准化学习流程、多传感器融合定位以及校准的推进器-水动力学模型与约束力分配算法，通过 ROS 2 构建完整的 Sim-to-Real 迁移管线，并验证了控制策略的可靠迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人学习依赖仿真器，理想仿真器需同时具备高保真水动力学、便捷的学习接口以及可信的真实场景迁移能力。然而现有平台往往难以兼顾这几方面，导致理论研究与实际应用之间存在较大鸿沟，因此需要一个面向 Sim-to-Real 的统一仿真平台。

Method: FinsSim 首先构建支持可选后端的高保真仿真以适应不同需求；提供标准控制基线和统一的机器人学习工作流；采用多传感器融合方案实现低成本且精确的定位以支持可靠迁移；实现经过校准的推进器-水动力学模型与带约束的力分配算法；并通过 ROS 2 将各模块桥接为完整的 Sim-to-Real 迁移管线。

Result: 通过匹配的仿真与真实实验，证明使用 FinsSim 框架能够实现水下机器人控制策略的可靠 Sim-to-Real 迁移；独立的消融研究进一步验证了各模块能够从不同方面解决水下 Sim-to-Real 的关键问题。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在弥合理论研究与实际应用之间的差距，推动水下机器人领域的进步。

Abstract: Underwater robot learning relies on simulators that integrate high-fidelity hydrodynamics, convenient learning interfaces, and a credible transition to real scenarios. In this work, we present FinsSim, a reality-aligned integrated simulation platform for Sim-to-Real underwater robot learning. FinsSim first constructs high-fidelity simulation with selectable backends to adapt to diverse requirements. To facilitate underwater robot research, it further offers standard control baselines, alongside with unified robot learning workflows. For reliable Sim-to-Real transfer, FinsSim adopts a multi-sensor fusion scheme to provide low-cost yet precise localization. Moreover, it implements calibrated thruster-hydrodynamics models and a constrained wrench allocation algorithm. Bridging these modules by ROS~2, FinsSim establishes a complete Sim-to-Real transfer pipeline. Through matched simulations and experiments, it is demonstrated that reliable Sim-to-Real transfer of underwater robot control policies can be achieved with the FinsSim framework. Separate ablation studies also validate that the modules of FinsSim can address the pivotal issues of underwater Sim-to-Real from different aspects. Overall, this work aims to bridge the gap between theoretical research and practical applications, ultimately driving advancements in the field of underwater robotics.

</details>


### [114] [Topology-Informed Visual Prompting For Vision Language Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23944)
*Haoyang Wu,Abhinav Kumar,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出拓扑引导的视觉提示框架，用Gauss-Linking-Integral拓扑签名捕获环境拓扑特性，通过仿真规划增强VLA微调数据，并微调VLM从实时观测预测签名与末端执行器路点作为视觉提示，在仿真双臂任务和真实抓取任务中超越基线，硬件成功率提升40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在具有复杂障碍几何的操控任务中因部分可观测性而表现不佳。这类几何结构会导致相似的视觉观测或机器人配置却需要性质不同的动作，该区别可用拓扑签名量化。具有完整环境几何与物体状态信息的运动规划器能推理这些签名，但部署时这些信息往往未知。

Method: 提出拓扑引导的视觉提示框架：利用基于仿真的规划增强标称演示数据集，并在部署时提供基于视觉的引导。采用Gauss-Linking-Integral拓扑签名表示捕获环境的重要拓扑属性；利用仿真近似环境的特权几何信息，用将系统移动到已演示签名并从新配置恢复任务执行的轨迹增强VLA微调数据集；在同一数据集上微调VLM，使其既能从实时相机观测预测签名，又能预测末端执行器路点，并将路点渲染为观测上的视觉提示以引导VLA。

Result: 在三个仿真双臂任务和一个真实世界箱子拾取任务中，该方法优于仅在标称演示上微调的VLA，以及可能移除拓扑相关信息观测的VLM提示基线。在硬件上，其任务成功率超过最强基线40%。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑签名表示与仿真规划数据增强，结合VLM预测签名和路点作为视觉提示，可有效缓解部分可观测性带来的复杂障碍操控难题，在仿真与真实硬件上均显著提升VLA任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can struggle with manipulation tasks with complex obstacle geometries due to partial observability. These complex geometries can lead to similar visual observations or robot configurations requiring qualitatively different actions, a distinction that can be quantified using topological signatures. While motion planners with full knowledge of environment geometries and object states can reason about these signatures in planning, this information is often not known at deployment. To address this issue, we present a topology-guided visual-prompting framework that uses simulation-based planning to augment a nominal demonstration dataset and provides vision-based guidance at deployment. Our method uses a Gauss-Linking-Integral topological signature representation to capture important topological properties of the environment. Using privileged geometry information from a simulation approximation of our environment, we augment a VLA fine-tuning dataset with trajectories that move the system to a demonstrated signature and, from the new configuration, resume task execution. A vision-language model (VLM) is fine-tuned on the same dataset to both predict signatures from live camera observations and predict end-effector waypoints, which are rendered as visual prompts on the observations to guide the VLA. Across three simulated bimanual tasks and a real-world box pickup task, our method outperforms a VLA fine-tuned only on nominal demonstrations and a VLM-prompting baseline that can remove topology-relevant information from observations. On hardware, it exceeds the strongest baseline by 40% in task success. Project website: https://topology-vla.github.io.

</details>


### [115] [Opt2VLA: Force-Aware Vision-Language-Action for Contact-Rich Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23968)
*Fukang Liu,Yipu Chen,Jaehwi Jang,Danfei Xu,Zsolt Kira,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Opt2VLA 提出一种力感知的人形机器人全身操作 VLA 框架，在 VLA 与控制器的接口上显式引入力指令，由单一多任务策略联合预测几何运动目标与连续接触力参考，并用任务特定的 RL 全身控制器跟踪，训练数据由带显式力参考的全身轨迹优化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人 VLA 系统主要以几何运动目标表示动作，依赖侧重运动跟踪的全身控制器，缺乏对交互力的显式推理与控制。在接触密集任务中，几何相似的动作可能因任务语境需要不同力区间，且接触后视觉观测可能不可靠，因此需要引入显式力调节能力。

Method: 提出 Opt2VLA：在 VLA 与控制的接口处显式引入力命令；单一多任务 VLA 策略同时预测几何运动目标与连续接触力参考；由任务特定的基于强化学习的全身控制器跟踪；通过带显式力参考的全身轨迹优化生成动态可行、接触一致的训练数据，提供可扩展且物理合理的监督信号。

Result: 在三个接触密集的人形机器人任务上评估：显式力条件化相比仅运动控制能实现更准确、更一致的力调节；来自轨迹优化的物理合理力矩监督进一步提升力跟踪精度与稳定性；闭环实验展示了在仿真和真实人形硬件上语言条件化的力调制能力。

Conclusion: Opt2VLA 表明将显式力命令引入人形 VLA 框架并结合轨迹优化的物理监督，可有效提升接触密集任务中的力调节精度与稳定性，并在仿真与真实硬件上实现语言条件化的力调制。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are expected to perform diverse human-level tasks in daily environments, many of which require precise regulation of interaction forces. While recent vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise for semantic planning and visuomotor control, existing humanoid systems primarily represent actions through geometric motion goals and rely on whole-body controllers focused on motion tracking, with limited explicit reasoning or control of interaction forces. This limitation is particularly relevant in contact-rich tasks, where geometrically similar motions may require different force regimes depending on the task context and where visual observations may become unreliable after contact. In this work, we present Opt2VLA, a force-aware VLA framework that introduces explicit force commands at the VLA-to-control interface for humanoid whole-body manipulation. A single multi-task VLA policy jointly predicts both geometric motion goals and continuous contact-force references, which are tracked by task-specific reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body controllers. To provide scalable and physically grounded supervision, we generate dynamically feasible and contact-consistent training data via whole-body trajectory optimization (TO) with explicit force references. We evaluate Opt2VLA on three contact-rich humanoid tasks and show that explicit force conditioning enables more accurate and consistent force regulation than motion-only control, while physically grounded torque supervision from TO further improves force tracking accuracy and stability. Closed-loop evaluations further demonstrate language-conditioned force modulation with Opt2VLA in simulation and on humanoid hardware.

</details>


### [116] [Anticipatory Robot Goalkeeping via Monotone Optimal Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23976)
*Hao E. Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yisen Li,Yaru Niu,Yikai Wang,Raihan Haque,Khalil Zbiss,Guanyang Luo,Hui-ping Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将机器人快速拦截中的"何时启动动作"决策建模为策略条件有限时域最优停止问题，提出单调最优停止（MOS）方法，通过直接Bellman递推学习"立即行动优于再等待一次观测"的收益优势，并仅对物理紧迫性施加单调性约束，在单交叉条件下得到带界近似误差的阈值释放边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速物理交互中的机器人常需在对手意图尚未完全明确时行动，如预测式守门。等待可获得更可靠的目标信息，却降低拦截的物理可行性；过早行动虽保持可达性，却需在不确定性下启动运动，二者存在根本权衡，需解决何时启动的决策问题。

Method: 将何时启动运动表述为给定闭环扑救控制器下的策略条件有限时域最优停止问题；提出单调最优停止（MOS），一种结构化释放时机方法。四足扑救策略用强化学习训练，MOS依据演化机器人状态与目标信念决定策略何时激活。它不预测释放时刻也不仅依赖置信度，而是学习"立即行动相对再等一次观测"的回报优势，推导该行动-等待边际的直接Bellman递推，并仅对物理紧迫性施加单调性，以反映拦截机会随时间的不可逆损失。

Result: 在单交叉条件下，MOS具有阈值释放边界且有界近似误差。大量仿真表明，MOS将平均扑救率从参数匹配的学习门控的67.7%提升至74.4%，反向扑救从52.1%提升至66.5%。真机实验进一步展示了对人类射门方向假动作的快速拦截与释放后方向修正能力。

Conclusion: MOS通过结构化最优停止框架有效解决了不确定性下的动态机器人拦截启动时机问题，兼顾动态高难度扑救的早期激活与后验观测变化时的闭环适应，仿真与真机实验均验证其优于参数匹配的学习门控方法。

Abstract: Robots engaged in fast physical interactions often need to act before the intent of another agent is fully known. Anticipatory goalkeeping illustrates this challenge. Waiting provides more reliable information about the target but reduces the physical opportunity for interception, whereas acting early preserves reachability but requires initiating motion under uncertainty. Given a fixed closed-loop save controller, we formulate the decision of when to initiate motion as a policy-conditional finite-horizon optimal stopping problem. Building on this formulation, we propose monotone optimal stopping (MOS), a structured release-timing method for dynamic robotic interception. The quadruped save policy is trained with reinforcement learning, while MOS determines when the policy should be activated from the evolving robot state and target belief. Rather than predicting a release time or relying on confidence alone, MOS learns the return advantage of acting now over waiting for one more observation. We derive a direct Bellman recursion for this act-versus-wait margin and impose monotonicity only with respect to physical urgency, reflecting the irreversible loss of interception opportunity as time elapses. This structure enables early activation for dynamically demanding saves while preserving closed-loop adaptation when later observations change the predicted target. Under a single-crossing condition, MOS admits a threshold release boundary with a bounded approximation error. Extensive simulation studies show that MOS improves the mean save rate from 67.7% to 74.4% over a parameter-matched learned gate and increases reversal saves from 52.1% to 66.5%. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate rapid interception and post-release direction correction under human shot-direction feints.

</details>


### [117] [RoboTalk: Learning Multi-Robot Communication and Coordination from Multimodal Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23997)
*Dorian Benhamou Goldfajn,Mason Nakamura,Saaduddin Mahmud,Justin Svegliato,Kyle H. Wray,Shlomo Zilberstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboTalk，一个用于训练小型视觉语言模型进行机器人协作沟通的合成数据生成流程与数据集，包含7,950条多模态轨迹、53个移动操作厨房任务，微调后模型在未见任务上成功率达77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协作在部分可观测条件下仍具挑战性，自然语言沟通是有前景的方案，但在去中心化操作中，联合学习显式机器人间沟通与技能级动作选择、并适配端侧部署的小型视觉语言模型这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出RoboTalk合成数据生成流程与数据集，含7,950条多模态轨迹、覆盖53个移动操作厨房任务；数据包含领导者-跟随者规划协议、工具调用（感知、操作、导航、沟通）、推理轨迹及多样化的自然语言沟通；用于微调开源小模型。

Result: 在未见新任务上，微调后的开源模型成功率达77%，显著优于未微调开源模型约2%的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过合成多模态数据微调小型视觉语言模型，可有效联合学习机器人间沟通与动作选择，大幅提升部分可观测下多机器人协作任务的成功率，为端侧部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-robot collaboration could enable more efficient and scalable solutions to complex robotic tasks, but collaboration under partial observability remains challenging. Natural-language communication offers a promising approach to coordinating robots under partial observability. However, in decentralized manipulation, jointly learning explicit inter-robot communication and skill-level action selection from multimodal demonstrations remains underexplored for small vision-language models (VLMs) intended for on-device deployment. To address this gap, we introduce RoboTalk, a synthetic data-generation pipeline and dataset of 7,950 multimodal trajectories spanning 53 mobile-manipulation kitchen tasks for training small VLMs to communicate and coordinate. The dataset includes a leader-follower planning protocol, tool calls (perception, manipulation, navigation, and communication), rationale traces, and diversified natural-language communication. Fine-tuning open-source models on our dataset can reach 77% success on novel held-out tasks, a significant improvement over the untuned open source models, which had a success rate of around ~2%.

</details>


### [118] [Imagine-RL: Residual-Confidence-Guided Cross-Attention for World-Model-Augmented VLA Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24033)
*Kejia Hu,Wentong Zhai,Bo Zhao,Shuai Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine-RL通过在噪声空间VLA后训练中引入动作条件化的视觉-力矩想象，让评论家超越当前观测评估候选动作的未来后果，仅用100条RL轨迹即在四个真实机器人任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中的可靠动作评估需要超越当前观测，考虑未来的视觉与接触后果。现有噪声空间强化学习虽能高效引导冻结的VLA策略，但其评论家基本忽略了这些未来后果。

Method: 提出Imagine-RL，在噪声空间VLA后训练中加入动作条件化的视觉-力矩想象。对每个候选动作块，冻结的视觉-力矩潜在世界模型（VTLWM）自回归预测紧凑未来表示，无需像素重建。当前图像-状态-动作查询对观测历史与预测未来进行注意力聚合，上一窗口预测残差提供逐token置信先验以抑制不可靠的未来token。结合当前证据与预测后果，动作评论家更好地评估候选动作并监督演员，同时VLA与VTLWM保持冻结。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务、每任务50次评估试验中，Imagine-RL仅使用100条RL轨迹，平均成功率相比DSRL提升23.6%，相比VLA基线提升60%。

Conclusion: 通过在动作评论中融入动作条件化的未来视觉-力矩想象与置信度加权，可在冻结VLA与世界模型的前提下显著提升接触丰富操作的后训练效率与成功率。

Abstract: Reliable action evaluation in contact-rich manipulation requires looking beyond the current observation to future visual and contact consequences. Existing noise-space reinforcement learning efficiently steers a frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, but its critics largely ignore these consequences. We present Imagine-RL, which augments noise-space VLA post-training with action-conditioned visual-torque imagination. For each candidate action chunk, a frozen visual-torque latent world model (VTLWM) autoregressively predicts compact future representations without pixel reconstruction. A current image-state-action query attends to observed histories and predicted futures, while previous-window prediction residuals provide token-wise confidence priors that suppress unreliable future tokens. By combining current evidence with predicted consequences, the action critic better evaluates candidate actions and supervises the actor, while the VLA and VTLWM remain frozen. Across four real-robot tasks with 50 evaluation trials per task, Imagine-RL uses only 100 RL trajectories and improves the average success rate by (23.6%) over DSRL and by (60%) over VLA baselines.

</details>


### [119] [What Matters in Designing World Action Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24048)
*Chao Tang,Haoqing Wang,Zilang Cen,Weishi Mi,Wei Xia,Fangcheng Liu,Anda Cheng,Yeqing Shen,Xiaohui Cui,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yehui Tang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对World Action Models（WAMs）的核心设计选择进行受控研究，拆解因果结构、潜空间表示与训练目标三大要素，系统比较其效果并提出设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM系统往往将架构、训练策略等多种设计选择打包为统一系统，难以隔离单个设计的贡献，也无法系统比较替代方案。

Method: 围绕三个基本问题开展受控实验：(1)世界建模与动作生成之间的因果结构；(2)世界建模应在何种潜空间进行；(3)不同世界-动作建模目标如何影响模型行为。在RoboCasa-GR1、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus三个基准上比较6种因果结构、8种潜表示、4种训练目标，并在DROID真实机器人数据上验证关键结论。

Result: 系统比较了现有WAM的主流设计选择，揭示了各设计要素对世界-动作建模的经验影响及其作用机理，关键发现在真实机器人数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 为WAM核心设计选择如何影响建模效果提供系统理解，并为未来WAM系统开发提供设计原则指导。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalizable robot control. Despite the growing number of WAM systems, existing works often introduce unified systems that bundle together multiple design choices, such as architecture and training strategy, making it difficult to isolate individual contributions and systematically compare alternative designs. In this work, we present a controlled study that disentangles these design choices and analyzes not only their empirical effects, but also how and why they shape WAMs. More specifically, we focus on three fundamental questions in building WAMs: (1) what causal structure should govern the interaction between world modeling and action generation? (2) in which latent space should world modeling be performed? and (3) how do different world-action modeling objectives affect model behavior and performance? Through structurally controlled experiments on three representative benchmarks, RoboCasa-GR1, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus, we systematically compare six causal structures, eight latent representations, and four training objectives, covering popular design choices in existing WAMs. We further validate our key findings on real-robot data from the DROID dataset. We hope to provide a systematic understanding of how core design choices affect world-action modeling and what principles can guide the development of future WAM systems.

</details>


### [120] [AquaOrbit: Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Underwater Target Orbiting under Intermittent Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24054)
*Kanzhong Yao,Jinyi Leng,Hao Zhang,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AquaOrbit强化学习控制器，通过恢复模块应对水下目标环绕中的间歇性视觉丢失，仿真与真机零样本部署均验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环绕任务中视觉反馈间歇丢失会破坏目标相对反馈，难以维持协调运动并重新捕获移动目标。

Method: AquaOrbit为带恢复模块的强化学习控制器：检测丢失时利用锁存的视线、横滚和深度参考维持稳定并重捕目标；在Isaac Sim中训练，注入动力学、观测与视觉丢失随机化。

Result: 在Gazebo/ROS2不同物理引擎与感知扰动下无需重训：静态与移动目标条件各20/20成功；移动目标下视线误差较带恢复的PID视觉伺服降低约46%，路径跟踪相当；移除恢复模块仅9/20。真机零样本完成椭圆、8字及变深圆周轨迹，可在8s遮挡下保持姿态稳定，并在2.5s内重捕目标。

Conclusion: 恢复模块对视觉丢失下的水下目标环绕至关重要，策略具备跨仿真与真机的泛化能力。

Abstract: Intermittent visual loss disrupts target-relative feedback during underwater orbiting, making it difficult to maintain coordinated motion and reacquire a moving target. We present AquaOrbit, a reinforcement-learning controller with a recovery module for underwater target orbiting under interrupted visual feedback. During detection loss, the recovery module uses latched line-of-sight, roll, and depth references to support stabilization and target reacquisition. We train the controller in Isaac Sim with dynamics, observation, and vision-loss randomization. Evaluated without retraining in Gazebo/ROS2 under a different physics engine and perception perturbations, AquaOrbit completes 20/20 orbiting trials in each of the static- and moving-target conditions on an unseen variable-depth 3-D trajectory. In the moving-target condition, it reduces mean line-of-sight error by approximately 46% relative to a PID-based visual servoing controller with recovery while maintaining comparable path-tracking accuracy; removing the recovery module reduces completion to 9/20. Zero-shot physical deployment with fully onboard perception and control demonstrates elliptical, figure-eight, and variable-depth circular trajectories, including the latter two trajectory types absent from training. The robot maintains attitude stability during manual occlusions lasting up to 8s and reacquires the target within 2.5s in the reported attitude-induced field-of-view loss events.

</details>


### [121] [Toward Human-in-the-Loop Robot Failure Recovery: Bridging Communication Gaps in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24055)
*Promise Ekpo,Teju Vijay,Dhruv Mandalik,Tisha Jain,Arman Ibrayeva,Sunishka Sil,Stefanie A. Tellex,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LD-HRI，一个用于评估机器人在存在听者知识差异时求助沟通能力的游戏、数据集与基准，通过人类听者表现来检验说话者，发现模型请求对新手更有效但仍有显著专家-新手差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人可通过向旁观者求助从失败中恢复，但有效的人机协同恢复需要沟通能考虑到人们知识差异。之前的逆语义工作使用单一听者模型生成请求，未检验听者知识差异，因此存在研究空白。

Method: 提出 LD-HRI（Listener Differences in Human-Robot Interaction），包含游戏、数据集与基准，通过人类听者表现评估说话者。评估考察请求属性、大语言模型（LLM）说话者以及逆语义请求选择算法，并在受控的听者信息差异下进行测试。语料包含 446 个人类书写请求和 1302 次听者试验，另评估 24 个冻结 LLM 书写请求与 70 名人类听者在 560 次试验中的表现。

Result: 在所有四个任务中，模型书写请求的新手成功率在描述性统计上更高；但两种请求来源都留下显著的专家-新手差距，其中 LLM 请求的差距达 16 个百分点。

Conclusion: LD-HRI 使这些差距变得可测量，为人机交互与人-智能体交互中设计更稳健的沟通提供了基础。

Abstract: Robots can recover from failures by asking bystanders for help, but effective human-in-the-loop recovery requires communication that accounts for differences in people's knowledge. Prior inverse-semantics work generates requests using a single listener model, leaving differences in listener knowledge untested. We introduce Listener Differences in Human-Robot Interaction (LD-HRI), a game, dataset, and benchmark that evaluates speakers through human listener performance. Our evaluation examines request properties, large language model (LLM) speakers, and inverse-semantics request-selection algorithms under controlled differences in listener information. The corpus contains 446 human-written requests and 1{,}302 listener trials. We additionally evaluated 24 frozen LLM-written requests with 70 human listeners across 560 trials. Novice success is descriptively higher with model-written requests across all four tasks, yet both request sources leave substantial expert--novice gaps, including 16 percentage points for LLM requests. LD-HRI makes these gaps measurable, providing a foundation for designing more robust communication in human-robot and human-agent interaction.

</details>


### [122] [Automatic Labelling for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24059)
*Yupu Lu,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动标注流水线，用确定性轨迹分析定位时间边界，用视觉语言推理生成语义描述，在29个真实双臂移动操作任务上验证了标注可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义化的子任务标签能为长时程策略提供有用上下文，但自动识别可靠的时间边界和广泛的语义描述仍然困难。

Method: 流水线将同步的运动学信号分割为阶段，进行阶段局部化的视觉语言（VL）推理以描述内容，并分别聚合基座、左臂和右臂动作的输出。

Result: 在29个Galaxea双臂移动操作任务上评估：重复VL推理三次在选定任务上输出值一致率为87.4%；九名参与者对所有29个任务的评审显示对时间划分（90.5%）、身体标签（90.7%）和手臂标签（78.7%）的正面接受度。

Conclusion: 分割-VL设计能产生结构化标注，同时保留异步双臂行为，为更丰富的语义子任务识别和基于状态的验证提供了基础。

Abstract: Semantically meaningful subtask labels can provide useful contexts for long-horizon policies, but automatically identifying both reliable temporal boundaries and broad semantic descriptions for annotations remains difficult. We present an automatic labelling pipeline that assigns temporal localisation to deterministic trajectory analysis and semantic interpretation to vision-language (VL) reasoning. The pipeline segments synchronised kinematic signals into phases, performs phase-localised VL reasoning to describe the contents, and aggregates the outputs for the base, left arm, and right arm actions. We evaluate this pipeline primarily on 29 real Galaxea bimanual mobile-manipulation tasks. Repeating the VL reasoning three times first produces the same output value for 87.4% on selected tasks. A review by nine participants across all 29 tasks then judgements on the labelled phases and shows positive acceptance of temporal divisions (90.5%), body labels (90.7%), and arm labels (78.7%). The results indicate that the segmentation-VL design can produce structured annotations while preserving asynchronous bimanual behaviour, providing a basis for richer semantic subtask identification and state-based verification.

</details>


### [123] [Safety Control of a Hyper-redundant Robot via Adaptive Weighted Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24062)
*Zijian Cai,Kiwan Wong,Wenci Xin,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出加权控制障碍函数（W-CBFs）框架，兼顾超冗余机器人安全约束与不均匀负载导致的跟踪误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冗余机器人适合受限空间操作，但杂乱环境安全操作困难，且细长结构导致负载分布不均与沿本体跟踪误差不均匀。

Method: 提出W-CBFs控制框架；先在圆形路径跟踪任务、不同障碍配置下评估（固定权重），再研究基于仿真与实验差异的自适应权重策略与映射函数。

Result: 固定权重下RMS跟踪误差最大降低：仿真59.6%、实验87.7%；自适应权重进一步降低21.9%与8.5%，但障碍贴近机器人时误差上升。清洁覆盖任务中自主策略覆盖性能与手动遥操作相当或更优，且避免碰撞，而手动操作发生碰撞。

Conclusion: W-CBFs可在保证安全的同时有效降低跟踪误差，自适应权重可进一步改善性能，自主策略在未针对覆盖优化的情况下仍具良好覆盖与安全性。

Abstract: Hyper-redundant robots are well suited for confined-space manipulation due to their high dexterity, but safe operation in cluttered environments remains challenging. In addition, their slender structures often lead to uneven load distributions and nonuniform tracking errors along the body. To address these issues, this work proposes a weighted control barrier functions (W-CBFs) framework that enforces safety constraints while reducing tracking errors caused by uneven loading. The proposed controller was first evaluated on a circular path-following task under different obstacle configurations. With fixed weights, compared to the non-weighted method, the maximum reduction in root-mean-square (RMS) tracking error was 59.6\% in simulation and 87.7\% in physical experiments. An adaptive weighting strategy was then investigated based on the discrepancy between simulated and experimental performance under different mapping functions. The RMS errors were further reduced by 21.9\% and 8.5\%, respectively, although the error increases when obstacles were located close to the robot body. Finally, the robot was evaluated in a cleaning task requiring coverage of a rectangular area and compared with manual teleoperation. Although the controller was not explicitly optimized for area coverage, the autonomous strategy achieved comparable or better coverage performance while avoiding collisions with the surrounding frame, whereas collisions occurred during manual operation.

</details>


### [124] [When Does Touch Matter? Charting the Vision-Interaction Gap in Cluttered Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24068)
*Hao Jiang,Luis Dominguez,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过真实世界受控实验，研究在杂乱环境中灵巧抓取时，触觉测量（扳手与触觉单元）何时优于纯视觉几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中灵巧抓取面临遮挡和接触问题，视觉几何难以判断抓取质量，需要评估交互信号（触觉与外部力/力矩估计）的增益，但缺乏对多种交互模态的受控、分离评估。

Method: 在五种桌面场景条件下开展真实世界受控研究，系统结合视觉、逐指与腕部扳手估计及分布式指尖触觉单元。固定演示、视觉观测、动作空间与柔顺控制，对比仅视觉、扳手、触觉单元及组合策略，并包含表征与融合基线，进行消融与行为对比。

Result: 组合策略在24/25次试验中成功，仅视觉为14/25；在三种受限条件下为15/15对比6/15。消融显示扳手与触觉反馈互补。行为对比显示交互反馈使不充分接触更早被拒绝、提拉前重新抓取、抓取更稳定。

Conclusion: 这是首个分别在真实世界中结合并评估这些交互模态用于杂乱环境中目标导向灵巧抓取的研究，揭示了视觉-交互差距的扩大，并将杂乱灵巧抓取定位为判断学习策略何时需要交互感知的基准。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in clutter poses a basic sensing question: when do tactile measurements and external wrench estimates improve on visual geometry? Occlusion and contact can obscure grasp quality, motivating a controlled evaluation of these interaction signals. We present a controlled real-world study over five tabletop scene conditions on a dexterous system that combines vision, per-finger and wrist wrench estimates, and distributed fingertip taxels. With demonstrations, visual observations, action space, and compliant control fixed, we compare vision-only, wrench, taxel, and combined policies plus representation and fusion baselines. The combined policy succeeds in 24/25 trials versus 14/25 for vision only, and 15/15 versus 6/15 across the three confined conditions. Ablations show that wrench and taxel feedback are complementary. Behavioral comparisons show that interaction feedback enables earlier rejection of inadequate contacts, regrasping before lift, and more stable grasps. To our knowledge, this is the first real-world study to combine and separately evaluate these interaction modalities for target-oriented dexterous grasping in clutter. These results chart a widening vision-interaction gap and position cluttered dexterous grasping as a benchmark for determining when the learned policy needs interaction sensing. Project website: https://interaction-dex-grasp.github.io/

</details>


### [125] [Dexterous Robot Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Contact-Anchored Retargeting and Residual Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24093)
*Zihao Yang,Chengyuan Liu,Yu Zhou,Runze Lv,Tianyu Cui,Sheng Yi,Haohua Zhu,Irvine Lu,JieQ Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个三阶段流程，将人类动作捕捉数据转化为灵巧机器人操作策略，无需真实机器人训练数据。核心思路是保留演示中的“接触结构”而非关节运动，并用一个残差强化学习策略统一修复物理一致性与动态可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习灵巧操作受限于数据：决定抓取成败的接触力在所有可扩展的人类演示来源中都缺失。作者基于两个观察：一是从人手到机器人手可迁移的是演示的接触结构（哪些手指区域接触哪些物体位置及顺序），而非关节运动；二是物理一致性无需逐任务设计，单个残差RL策略可跨多样演示训练一次，将运动学记录修复为物理一致且带接触标注的轨迹，同一残差形式也能在重定向后恢复动态可行性。

Method: 三阶段流程：1）物理细化——用模拟MANO手恢复接触与力；2）接触锚定重定向——通过一个与手部形态无关的目标迁移所演示的接触结构；3）残差策略学习——使结果适应机器人驱动。

Result: 重建25,454条单手轨迹（成功率7.3%→59.3%）与25个双手任务（16.0%→62.4%），每个设置仅用一份共享策略；将一个人类数据集迁移到四种形态不同的机器人手（+62.4个百分点）；在物理硬件上执行四个接触丰富的双手任务，零真实机器人训练数据。

Conclusion: 以接触结构为核心的表示与统一的残差RL修复机制，可实现从人类演示到多种机器人手的灵巧操作策略迁移，且无需真实机器人训练数据。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from demonstrations is bottlenecked by data: the contact forces that determine whether a grasp succeeds are absent from every scalable source of human demonstrations. This paper builds on two observations. First, what survives the change from a human hand to a robot hand is the contact structure of a demonstration - which finger regions touch which object locations, and in what order - rather than its joint motion. Second, physical consistency need not be engineered per task: a single residual reinforcement learning (RL) policy, trained once across diverse demonstrations, can repair kinematic recordings into physically consistent, contact-annotated trajectories, and the same residual formulation restores dynamic feasibility after retargeting. These observations yield a three-stage pipeline that converts human motion-capture recordings into dexterous robot policies with no real-robot training data: physics refinement with a simulated MANO hand recovers contacts and forces, contact-anchored retargeting transfers the demonstrated contact structure through an objective independent of hand morphology, and residual policy learning adapts the result to robot actuation. The pipeline reconstructs 25,454 single-hand trajectories (success 7.3% -> 59.3%) and 25 dual-hand tasks (16.0% -> 62.4%) with one shared policy per setting, transfers one human dataset to four morphologically distinct robot hands (+62.4 pp), and executes four contact-rich bimanual tasks on physical hardware with zero real-robot training data.

</details>


### [126] [Ask Before It Tells: Benchmark-to-Robot Body-Cue Transfer for a Question-First Bedside Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24099)
*Dongsik Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出床边机器人原型 Nuni，把检测到的痛苦线索当作"询问的理由"而非"报警的理由"，并用姿态中心的混合管线与两种 X3D-UGT RGB 分类器进行初步技术对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准数据集上的高准确率并不保证机器人在机器人相机视角下可靠运行，因为视角与场景差异可能导致外观模型失效。因此需要一个在交互层面能降低感知不确定后果的方案。

Method: 构建 Nuni 床边机器人原型，采用"先询问"的交互控制策略；对比两种 X3D-UGT RGB 外观分类器（微调版与从头训练版）与一种姿态中心混合管线，在 28 个机器人相机录制的单演员脚本化片段上评估六类识别；并通过事件注入测试状态转换控制器，共 13 次试验。

Result: RGB 分类器在 NTU RGB+D 上六类准确率分别为 97.7% 与 94.8%，但在机器人视角片段上仅得 0.25 与 0.29 六类宏召回；混合管线达 0.71。混合管线在 16 个痛苦片段中 12 个触发询问，8 个正常片段中 2 个会不必要询问；RGB 变体仅 2/16 和 3/16。控制器 13 次状态转换试验全部通过：有效回应触发解除，两次未应答提示产生一次报警，三个边界条件均正确处理。

Conclusion: 这是初步技术评估而非用户研究或医学验证，但结果表明交互策略能够限制不确定感知所带来的后果，为辅助机器人提供了"先询问后报警"的可信设计方向。

Abstract: Body-cue recognition can support assistive robots, but benchmark accuracy does not guarantee reliable behavior under a robot-camera viewpoint. We present Nuni, a bedside robot prototype that treats a detected distress cue as a reason to ask rather than a reason to alert. We compare two X3D-UGT RGB appearance classifiers, which reach 97.7% and 94.8% six-way accuracy on NTU RGB+D, with a pose-centric hybrid pipeline on 28 single-actor scripted clips recorded from the robot camera. The hybrid path achieved 0.71 six-way macro recall, versus 0.25 and 0.29 for the fine-tuned and from-scratch RGB variants. More importantly for interaction, it produced a question-triggering distress cue in 12/16 distress clips and would have prompted unnecessarily in 2/8 normal clips; the RGB variants yielded a question-triggering cue in only 2/16 and 3/16 distress clips. We separately tested the question-first controller through event injection. All 13 state-transition trials passed: valid responses caused stand-down, two unanswered prompts produced one alert, and three boundary conditions were handled correctly. These results are a preliminary technical evaluation, not a user study or medical validation, but they show how interaction policy can limit the consequences of uncertain perception.

</details>


### [127] [CARE: Experience-Guided Atomic Corrective Execution for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24118)
*Junlan Xiao,Junwei Jiang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Zhongbo Zhang,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过学习执行中的失败来提升VLA策略的恢复能力，并构建FSR-Bench基准，在仿真与真实双臂任务中分别取得14.5和15.9个点的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在机器人操作中表现优异，但一旦执行偏离标称轨迹就变得脆弱，现有纠错数据多来自人工设计或随机扰动，缺乏对真实失败分布的刻画。

Method: 不同于从人工设计或随机扰动生成纠错数据，CARE收集失败rollout，对分阶段的失败后偏差进行建模，用得到的经验分布合成代表性失败状态与纠错示范；推理时结合阶段式规划与基于物理的3D监控，触发原子级调整或重操作，同时保留任务进度。还提出FSR-Bench基准，评估在局部偏差和结构性异常下从中间失败状态的恢复能力。

Result: 在多种VLA骨干、仿真基准和真实双臂任务上实验，效果一致提升，仿真平均任务成功率提升14.5个点，真实世界提升15.9个点。

Conclusion: 从真实失败中学习并配合阶段化监控与原子调整，可有效增强VLA策略的执行鲁棒性与失败恢复能力；代码、模型和数据已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain brittle once execution deviates from nominal trajectories. We propose CARE (Corrective Atomic Robotic Execution), a framework that improves recovery by learning from failures encountered during execution. Instead of generating corrective data from manually designed or random perturbations, CARE collects failed rollouts, models stage-conditioned post-failure deviations, and uses the resulting empirical distributions to synthesize representative failure states and corrective demonstrations. At inference time, CARE combines stage-wise planning with physically grounded 3D monitoring to trigger atomic adjustments or re-operations while preserving task progress. We further introduce the Failure State Recovery Benchmark (FSR-Bench), which evaluates recovery from intermediate failure states under local deviations and structural anomalies. Experiments across multiple VLA backbones, simulation benchmarks, and real-world dual-arm tasks show consistent improvements, with average task-success gains of 14.5 points in simulation and 15.9 points in the real world. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/xiaojunlan/care

</details>


### [128] [ActiveArena: Benchmarking and Understanding Active Perception in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24124)
*Yibo Li,Enshen Zhou,Rui Chen,Yanjun Ding,Mengzhen Liu,Yi Han,Jiabo Zhan,Lipeng Wang,Shanghang Zhang,Lu Sheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActiveArena，包含可主动感知的仿真器 ActiveArena-Sim、涵盖 35 项任务的基准 ActiveArena-Bench，以及 13 种 VLA 配置套件 ActiveArena-VLA，系统评估机器人在主动感知与操作中的记忆获取、写入与推理能力，并揭示明显的 ID-OOD 泛化差距与改进因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以评估机器人如何以主动方式有效获取并维护记忆中的信息，缺乏可控制视角、大规模工作空间与记忆标注的统一测试平台，因此需要构建面向主动感知与操作的评测基准。

Method: 构建 ActiveArena-Sim 仿真器（支持可控视角与大规模工作空间）；基于其设计 ActiveArena-Bench，包含 5 大类 35 项任务，覆盖视觉探索与交互式信息获取，强调多轮证据收集与基于记忆的推理，并提供记忆标注、标准化训练数据及 ID/OOD 协议；提出 ActiveArena-VLA 模块化套件，含 13 种视觉-语言-动作配置，用于可控研究记忆写入、记忆容量、本体状态、子任务监督与高层规划。

Result: 基准结果显示出显著的 ID-OOD 差距：均匀记忆采样、可靠写入策略下增大记忆容量、本体感知输入与子任务监督均能提升 OOD 泛化；规划器引导的记忆管理与决策在仅使用稀疏记忆时可达到接近最佳配置的性能。

Conclusion: ActiveArena 为主动感知与操作模型的开发与诊断提供了统一测试平台，有助于理解记忆机制与规划在泛化中的作用。

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate how robots effectively acquire and maintain information in memory in an active manner. To this end, we introduce ActiveArena-Sim, an active-perception simulator with controllable viewpoints and large-scale workspaces as the foundation. Built on this, we propose ActiveArena-Bench, which comprises 35 tasks across 5 fine-grained categories, covering visual exploration and interactive information acquisition. Each task is difficult to solve from passive observations alone, requiring multi-round evidence acquisition and memory-based reasoning. The benchmark provides rich memory annotations, standardized training data, and ID/OOD protocols featuring disjoint scenes, unseen distractor configurations, and novel backgrounds. Moreover, we present ActiveArena-VLA, a modular suite of 13 vision-language-action configurations for controlled studies of memory writing, memory capacity, proprioceptive state, subtask supervision, and high-level planning in active perception. Benchmark results reveal a substantial ID-OOD gap: uniform memory sampling, increased memory capacity under reliable write policies, proprioceptive inputs, and subtask supervision improve OOD generalization, while planner-guided memory management and decision-making achieve performance close to the best-performing configuration using only sparse memory.
  ActiveArena thus provides a unified testbed to develop and diagnose models for active perception and manipulation.

</details>


### [129] [Phrase-Level Robotic Guqin Performance: Bimanual Motion Planning and Audio-Tactile Interaction Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24133)
*Zhen Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Tianyuan Bao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异构双臂机器人系统，通过分层规划框架实现古琴乐句级自主演奏，结合视觉定位、触觉监测与听觉反馈校准，在25事件乐句实验中达到93.6%和96.8%的事件正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐器演奏是人形机器人与具身智能领域的高难度基准任务，要求无碰撞轨迹、精确接触时序、不对称双臂协调以及真实声学结果。古琴作为七弦无品乐器，具有毫米级弦距、右手瞬时拨弦与左手持续按音等独特挑战，现有研究尚未实现其乐句级自主演奏。

Method: 将古琴演奏建模为离散—连续混合执行问题，提出分层规划框架，统一协调工作手指分配、构型连续性、避障以及连续音乐事件间的紧耦合双臂接触时序；系统集成视觉引导的乐器定位、基于触觉的按音接触监测，以及听觉反馈驱动的拨弦参数标定。

Result: 在真实古琴上对包含25个事件的乐句进行实验，系统可靠完成散音与七徽按音序列的协调演奏，重复试验中事件正确率分别达到93.6%和96.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了物理异构双臂系统在古琴乐句级自主演奏中的可行性，为机器人精细乐器操作与高精度接触时序控制提供了可借鉴的分层规划与多模态反馈框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics and embodied intelligence have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex manipulation tasks. However, musical instrument performance remains a formidable benchmark, demanding not only collision-free trajectory execution but also precise contact timing, asymmetric bimanual coordination, and target acoustic outcomes on physical instruments. The guqin, a seven-string fretless zither, presents unique manipulation challenges due to its millimetric string spacing, transient right-hand plucking, and sustained left-hand harmonic contacts. In this work, we present a physical heterogeneous dual-arm robotic system for phrase-level autonomous guqin performance. We formulate guqin playing as a hybrid discrete--continuous execution problem and develop a hierarchical planning framework that coordinates working finger assignment, configuration continuity, obstacle avoidance, and tight bimanual contact schedules across consecutive musical events. The system integrates vision-guided instrument localization, tactile-based harmonic contact monitoring, and auditory feedback-informed plucking parameter calibration. Real-world experiments on a 25-event phrase demonstrate that the system reliably executes coordinated open-string and seventh-hui harmonic sequences on a physical guqin, achieving 93.6% and 96.8% event correctness across repeated trials.

</details>


### [130] [BayesianGS-SLAM: Uncertainty-Aware Neural Rendering SLAM via Probabilistic Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24140)
*Kyeongsu Kang,Seongbo Ha,Sibaek Lee,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 BayesianGS-SLAM，一个不确定性感知的 3D 高斯泼溅 SLAM 框架，对颜色和深度预测不确定性进行建模，并将其贯穿应用于建图、跟踪和关键帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经渲染 SLAM 依赖渲染的 RGB-D 残差进行相机跟踪与地图优化，但由于传感器噪声、观测覆盖有限和地图表示不完整，预测可靠性差异较大。缺乏显式可靠性估计时，不可靠残差会损害位姿优化，而已被地图充分解释的帧又会引发冗余建图更新。

Method: 提出可处理概率公式，将传感器噪声不确定性与不透明度诱导的地图表示不确定性结合，并通过渲染过程传播；所得预测不确定性用于增强建图、通过鲁棒位姿目标归一化跟踪残差，以及基于预测惊讶度的关键帧判据评估新帧。

Result: 在真实 RGB-D 数据集上，深度不确定性-误差排序相较现有不确定性感知 SLAM 方法显著提升；关键帧选择策略在保持有竞争力的跟踪与渲染性能的同时，减少了关键帧数量和建图调用次数。

Conclusion: 与仅考虑颜色不确定性或仅在建图中使用不确定性的已有方法不同，本框架为颜色和深度同时估计预测不确定性，并统一整合进建图、跟踪和关键帧选择，提升了系统可靠性并降低冗余计算。

Abstract: Neural-rendering-based SLAM relies on rendered RGB-D residuals for camera tracking and map optimization, but the reliability of these predictions can vary substantially because of sensor noise, limited observation coverage, and incomplete map representations. Without an explicit reliability estimate, unreliable residuals may adversely affect pose optimization, while frames already well explained by the current map may trigger redundant mapping updates. In this paper, we present BayesianGS-SLAM, an uncertainty-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that estimates predictive color and depth uncertainty during mapping and consistently reuses it across the SLAM pipeline. Our tractable probabilistic formulation combines a sensor-noise uncertainty component with an opacity-induced map-representation component propagated through the rendering process. The resulting predictive uncertainty is used to augment mapping, normalize tracking residuals through a robust pose objective, and evaluate incoming frames using a predictive-surprise-based keyframe criterion. Unlike prior uncertainty-aware neural-rendering SLAM methods that primarily consider color uncertainty or use uncertainty only during mapping, our framework estimates predictive uncertainty for both color and depth and integrates it into mapping, tracking, and keyframe selection. Evaluations on real-world RGB-D datasets demonstrate substantially improved depth uncertainty-error ranking compared with existing uncertainty-aware SLAM methods. Moreover, the proposed keyframe-selection strategy reduces the number of selected keyframes and mapping calls while maintaining competitive tracking and rendering performance.

</details>


### [131] [MimicAgent: Quadruped Skills via Prompt-to-Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24145)
*Lucky Kant Nayak,Narayanan Palghat Parameswaran,Neehar Peri,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicAgent 是一个"提示到轨迹"生成框架，用编码智能体根据技能提示生成四足动物参考轨迹，再以示例引导强化学习训练可在仿真与真实世界部署的动态四足技能；在智能体外壳下提示 Claude Fable 5.1 时，87% 的提示能生成语义对齐的参考轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足策略训练中奖励塑形虽被广泛使用，但奖励地形难以驾驭，往往需要数小时的人工试错。Eureka 等用 LLM 自动设计奖励，却难以跨技能与形态泛化。作者的观察是：让人类（乃至 LLM）生成参考运动远比设计奖励函数容易，这受类人机器人示例引导 RL 成功的启发，而四足动物缺乏此类参考运动数据，因此需要新的生成途径。

Method: 提出 MimicAgent，一个智能体式框架：给定技能提示，由编码智能体生成四足参考轨迹；这些粗略参考轨迹随后用于训练示例引导的强化学习策略，并部署于仿真和真实世界。

Result: 生成的参考轨迹可支撑示例引导 RL 策略训练，策略可在仿真与真实环境中部署；在智能体框架内使用 Claude Fable 5.1 时，87% 的提示产生了语义对齐的参考轨迹。

Conclusion: 相比自动化奖励设计，生成参考运动是更易泛化的路径；MimicAgent 为缺乏参考运动数据的四足动物动态技能学习提供了可行的提示到轨迹方案。

Abstract: We present MimicAgent, a prompt-to-trajectory generation framework for learning dynamic quadruped skills. Although reward shaping is extensively used when training quadruped policies, navigating the resulting reward landscape is notoriously difficult, requiring hours of "graduate student descent". Eureka attempts to automate reward design with LLMs, but we find that it struggles to generalize across diverse skills and morphologies. Our key observation is that it is far easier for a human - and by association, an LLM - to generate reference motions than to shape reward functions. Our hypothesis is motivated by the success of example-guided RL for humanoids, which exploits large-scale motion capture datasets as references for training locomotion policies. Unlike humanoids, quadrupeds lack such reference motion data. Towards this end, we propose MimicAgent, an agentic harness that, given a skill prompt, generates quadruped reference trajectories with coding agents. These coarse reference trajectories are then used to train example-guided RL policies that are deployable in simulation and in the real-world. Notably, we find that when prompting Claude Fable 5.1 within our agentic harness, 87% of prompts yield semantically aligned reference trajectories.

</details>


### [132] [Object-Centric Conditioning for Visuomotor Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24155)
*Jijie Li,Xu Yang,Junhong Zou,Chunhai Zhao,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhen Lei,Xiangyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SlotFlow，一种以物体为中心的流匹配策略，通过解耦语义与空间线索提升视觉运动操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有A2A流匹配虽提升推理效率，但陈旧的历史运动模式与纠缠的全局视觉表征，会在空间分布外偏移和视觉干扰下降低鲁棒性。

Method: SlotFlow将场景观测解耦为语义（"what"）特征与轻量级图像平面空间（"where"）线索，实现物体感知的策略条件化与当前状态锚定；语义表示抑制无关背景关联，空间线索提升对物体配置变化的适应能力。

Result: 大量仿真与真实实验表明，在视觉干扰和严重空间扰动下鲁棒性提升，同时保持A2A的低步数推理效率；控制初始化与感知消融实验显示，以物体为中心的锚定是主要增益来源，且与有用的历史运动先验互补而非替代。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的解耦表征可有效增强流匹配策略的鲁棒性，并与历史先验协同发挥作用。

Abstract: Robot visuomotor policies are commonly formulated as autoregressive, diffusion-based, or more recently, flow matching models. Among them, Action-to-Action (A2A) flow matching improves inference efficiency by initializing generation from historical action priors rather than stochastic noise. However, stale historical motion patterns and entangled global visual representations can jointly reduce robustness under spatial out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts and visual distractors. In this work, we propose SlotFlow, an object-centric flow matching policy for robust visuomotor manipulation. SlotFlow decouples scene observations into semantic ("what") features and lightweight image-plane spatial ("where") cues to provide object-aware policy conditioning and current-state grounding. The semantic representation suppresses irrelevant background correlations, while the spatial cue improves adaptation to shifted object configurations. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved robustness under visual distractors and severe spatial perturbations while preserving the low-step inference efficiency of A2A. Controlled initialization and perception ablations further identify object-centric grounding as a major source of the gains and show that it complements, rather than replaces, useful historical motion priors.

</details>


### [133] [GraspTune: Tactile-Driven Execution Refinement for Robust Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24180)
*Juntao Li,Xingke Xia,Sichao Liu,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspTune 是一个触觉驱动的执行阶段精化框架，通过在接近、接触形成和最终抓取过程中施加有界残余 TCP 运动，将视觉可行但物理不稳定的抓取提案转化为稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉抓取提案生成进展迅速，但将选定提案转化为稳定物理抓取仍是执行阶段的核心挑战。该文旨在解决视觉合理提案在执行时不稳定、难以直接落地的问题。

Method: GraspTune 从名义提案出发，在接近、接触形成和最终执行阶段施加有界残余 TCP 运动；利用局部深度、触觉信号、状态和历史学习面向控制的接触语义，采用状态条件专家接触查询以及接触变化、接触风险和闭合后就绪度的多任务监督；该表征条件化扩散预训练残差策略，并与 PPO 对齐实现闭环执行。

Result: 在 20 类物体、超过 60,000 次仿真执行中，GraspTune 对 GraspNet、Contact-GraspNet、AnyGrasp 和 VGN 四个提案生成器的稳定抓取成功率分别提升 +19.22、+9.55、+12.45 和 +20.70 个百分点。四折留出类别研究中，未见物体执行率从 54.58% 提升至 70.33%。在 UR5e 与 Xense 指尖传感器的 1,000 多次真实机器人试验中，GraspNet 执行率从 71.0% 提升至 84.3%，无需真实世界策略微调即可直接迁移。

Conclusion: GraspTune 能有效将视觉上看似合理的抓取提案转化为稳定物理抓取，为下游接触丰富操作提供支持，并在多种提案生成器和真实机器人场景中验证了执行层收益与泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual grasp proposal generation has advanced rapidly, yet converting a selected proposal into a stable physical grasp remains a central execution-stage challenge. This paper introduces GraspTune, a tactile-driven execution-stage refinement framework that starts from a nominal proposal and applies bounded residual TCP motions during approach, contact formation, and final grasp execution. GraspTune learns control-facing contact semantics from local depth, tactile signals, state, and history using state-conditioned expert contact queries and multi-task supervision for contact change, contact risk, and post-close readiness. The representation conditions a diffusion-pretrained residual policy and is aligned with PPO for closed-loop execution. Across more than 60,000 simulated executions over 20 object categories, GraspTune establishes an execution-layer benefit across four proposal generators, raising stable grasp success by +19.22, +9.55, +12.45, and +20.70 percentage points for GraspNet, Contact-GraspNet, AnyGrasp, and VGN. A four-fold held-out category study raises unseen-object execution from 54.58% to 70.33%, showing category-disjoint generalization of contact correction. Across more than 1,000 real-robot trials on a UR5e setup with Xense fingertip sensors, GraspTune raises GraspNet execution from 71.0% to 84.3%, validating direct transfer without realworld policy fine-tuning. Together, these results turn visually plausible proposals into stable physical grasps for downstream contact-rich manipulation. A supplementary video is available at https://youtu.be/kcq7fSLNtzU.

</details>


### [134] [StenoVLA-3D: 3D-Aware Reasoning VLA for Navigation Through Gastrointestinal Stenoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24187)
*Tamima Tabassum,Yiming Huang,Tianchun Wu,Changjing Liu,Zhiqing Tang,Chikit Ng,Beilei Cui,Liangjing Shao,Jiewen Lai,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3D感知VLA框架StenoVLA-3D，用于狭窄区域自主内镜导航，通过点图融合与时序状态建模提升几何定位与病灶报告能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主内镜导航要求策略模型能从纹理贫乏的单目观测预测动作、做出安全控制决策，并在病灶离开视野后仍保留其证据。现有VLA模型主要依赖视觉外观与短期上下文，限制了几何定位和整段（episode）级别的报告能力。

Method: 将点图（point-maps）通过可学习的几何门控融合集成进Cosmos-Reason 2骨干网络；提出时序状态分支建模穿越进度；推理-动作骨干在预测动作的同时给出有依据的推理，并由专门的头部分别估计狭窄形状和生成最终病灶报告；同时构建EndoCausal数据集（含病灶标注、动作和时序有据推理）。

Result: 在40个留存的录制测试episode上达到95.2%语义准确率和83.4%动作准确率；在物理3自由度内镜上，食管与结肠体模任务成功率分别为88.9%和77.8%（各36次试验），显著优于所评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: StenoVLA-3D通过3D几何感知与时序状态建模，有效提升内镜狭窄区域导航的语义与动作表现及真实体模任务成功率。

Abstract: Autonomous endoscopic navigation requires the policy model to predict actions from texture-poor monocular observations, make safe control decisions, and retain evidence of lesions after they leave the field of view. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) models primarily rely on visual appearance and short-term context, limiting geometric grounding and episode-level reporting. We introduce StenoVLA-3D, a 3D-aware VLA framework for navigating through stenotic regions. We integrate point-maps into the Cosmos-Reason 2 backbone through learned geometry-gated fusion, and also propose a temporal state branch to model traversal progress. Our reasoning-and-action backbone predicts grounded reasoning with actions, while dedicated heads estimate stenosis shape and generate the final lesion report. We further introduce EndoCausal, an episode-level dataset with lesion annotations, actions, and temporally grounded reasoning. On 40 held-out recorded test episodes, StenoVLA-3D reaches 95.2\% semantic accuracy and 83.4\% action accuracy. On the physical 3-DoF endoscope, it attains 88.9\% and 77.8\% task success in esophageal and colonic phantoms (36 trials each), substantially outperforming the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [135] [A Topological Representation with Object-Path Graphs for Open-Vocabulary Instance Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24189)
*Linwei Zheng,Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"对象—路径图"表示，将开放词汇语义推理与拓扑导航统一于单一轻量框架中，并基于该图设计了结合全局路径规划与节点间局部执行的导航策略，无需稠密度量地图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法通常将导航分解为序列化的语言引导决策，或依赖无先验环境知识的在线探索；场景图虽提供紧凑语义记忆，却与下游导航解耦，导航仍依赖稠密度量地图。为弥合这一鸿沟，作者提出统一语义与导航的图表示。

Method: 提出对象—路径图，联合支持语义落地、基于图的定位与导航；在同一拓扑框架内引入导航策略：全局路径规划 + 通过轻量节点定位与语义视觉伺服实现的节点间局部执行，从而直接在图上导航，无需稠密度量重建。

Result: 在 HM3D 与 Replica 数据集上，所提层次化图结构在开放词汇物体落地任务中取得有竞争力的性能，同时实现有效的导航表现；真实机器人实验进一步验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 对象—路径图可将开放词汇语义推理与拓扑导航有效统一，在仿真与真实环境中均能实现有效导航，证明无需稠密度量地图的图内导航方案具有可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires embodied agents to navigate environments using natural language instructions and visual observations. Existing approaches typically decompose navigation into sequential language-guided decisions or rely on online exploration without prior environmental knowledge. Scene graph representations offer compact semantic memory but remain decoupled from downstream navigation, which still depends on dense metric maps. To close this gap, we propose an object--path graph that unifies open-vocabulary semantic reasoning with topological navigation. The proposed representation jointly supports semantic grounding, graph-based localization, and navigation within a single lightweight topological framework. Building on this graph, we introduce a navigation strategy that combines global path planning with local inter-node execution through lightweight node localization and semantic visual servoing, enabling navigation directly over the graph without dense metric reconstruction. Experiments on HM3D and Replica demonstrate competitive performance in open-vocabulary object grounding through the proposed hierarchical graph structure, while achieving effective navigation performance. Real-world robot experiments further validate the practicality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [136] [Odometry-Aided Real-Time Mapping for Underwater Robots Using Forward-Looking Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24195)
*Siyuan Du,Kanzhong Yao,Youdong Wang,Yingqi Liu,Qingwen Liu,Qunhui Yang,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出级联特征重建流水线（FFT去噪、快速多尺度CFAR检测、梯度自适应边界连接）并结合姿态感知几何投影与增量占据累积，构建2.5D深度参考地图，实现低延迟实时FLS建图，泳池实验中平面建图RMSE低于3 cm，单帧平均处理时间42.4 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下复杂环境中光衰减与散射严重降低可见度，光学传感受限；前视声纳（FLS）虽能在弱光条件下提供高帧率声学成像，但实时FLS建图仍受目标高度未解、空间非均匀噪声和破碎目标边界三大问题困扰，导致特征提取困难与投影几何歧义。

Method: 提出级联特征重建流水线：基于FFT的去噪、快速多尺度恒虚警率（MCFAR）检测、梯度自适应边界连接，从退化声纳图像中低延迟提取几何特征；整合姿态感知几何投影与增量占据累积，构建深度参考的2.5D地图用于受限水下环境的局部建图。声纳垂直位置由外部传感器提供参考，目标高度在显式几何假设下赋值而非由FLS直接测量。

Result: 在3 m × 5 m泳池实验中，三个序列均实现厘米级平面建图精度，均方根误差（RMSE）低于3 cm，平均每帧处理时间为42.4 ms。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够从退化声纳图像中高效提取几何特征并完成低延迟2.5D局部建图，在受限水下环境中达到厘米级平面精度；但目标高度依赖显式几何假设与外部传感器参考，而非FLS直接测量。

Abstract: Reliable perception is essential for underwater vehicles operating in complex environments, where light attenuation and scattering often degrade visibility and compromise optical sensing. Forward-looking sonar (FLS) offers an alternative by providing high-frame-rate acoustic imaging under poor optical conditions. However, real-time FLS mapping remains challenging due to unresolved target elevation, spatially non-uniform noise, and fragmented target boundaries, which hinder feature extraction and introduce geometric ambiguity during projection. To address these challenges, we propose a cascaded feature reconstruction pipeline combining fast Fourier transform (FFT)-based denoising, fast multiscale constant false alarm rate (MCFAR) detection, and gradient-adaptive boundary connection to extract geometric features from degraded sonar images with low latency. We integrate attitude-aware geometric projection with incremental occupancy accumulation to construct a depth-referenced 2.5D map for local mapping in confined underwater environments. The sonar's vertical position is referenced to an external sensor, while target elevation is assigned under an explicit geometric assumption rather than measured directly by FLS. Experiments in a 3 m X 5 m pool demonstrate centimeter-scale planar mapping accuracy, with a root-mean-square error (RMSE) below 3 cm across three sequences and an average processing time of 42.4 ms per frame.

</details>


### [137] [Audio-based UAV Localization with Adaptive Temporal Correspondence via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24218)
*Haoxiang Lei,Mingzheng Feng,Daotong Wang,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应时间对应的音频定位框架，用探针片段提取声学状态，由强化学习控制器动态决定音频窗口大小，再经Mamba定位网络估计3D位置，实现低时延高精度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频定位方法依赖预定义的固定音频片段长度，限制了时间对应关系，并在"充足声学证据"与"及时定位"之间形成权衡（trade-off）。

Method: 1) 探针片段（probe segment）提取紧凑声学状态，刻画观测的可靠性与一致性；2) 由该状态引导的强化学习控制器动态确定每次定位决策所需的音频窗口大小；3) 所选音频片段输入基于Mamba的定位网络，通过自适应时间特征调制进行3D位置估计。

Result: 大量实验表明，相比SOTA方法，本方法在取得有竞争力的3D定位精度的同时，显著降低了时间对应时延，并在多种场景下表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自适应时间对应机制可有效缓解固定窗口的精度-时延权衡问题，为反无人机音频预警提供了低时延且泛化性强的3D定位方案。

Abstract: Audio-based localization provides a low-cost and illumination-independent sensing solution for anti-UAV early warning. However, existing methods typically rely on a predefined fixed audio segment length, which limits temporal correspondence and creates a trade-off between sufficient acoustic evidence and timely localization. To address this issue, we propose an audio-based localization framework with adaptive temporal correspondence. A probe segment is first used to extract a compact acoustic state that characterizes the reliability and consistency of the observation. Guided by the state, a reinforcement learning controller dynamically determines the required audio window size for each localization decision. The selected audio segment is then processed by a Mamba-based localization network with adaptive temporal feature modulation for 3D position estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive 3D localization accuracy with substantially reduced temporal correspondence latency compared to SOTA methods and exhibits strong generalization across scenarios.

</details>


### [138] [OpenFlyScan: A Quality-Guided Aerial Reconstruction System for Consumer Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24253)
*Zhongrui You,Zhen Li,Junli Liu,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenFlyScan 是一套面向消费级无人机的质量引导空中重建系统，通过 GS 质量模型预测重建质量、重采规划器生成补充采集条带，并借助定制移动 App 执行采集，实现低成本、快速的城市资产重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 能提供高保真的仿真场景，但大规模城市场景的资产构建受限于昂贵设备和滞后的质量反馈。预设航测可能对复杂表面观测不足，缺陷往往在重建后才发现，导致需返场重拍和重复处理。因此需要一种低成本、可提前预测质量并指导补采的方案。

Method: 系统包含三个核心部分：（1）GS 质量模型，从 GS 渲染误差中学习，预测各区域的重建质量；（2）重采规划器，基于质量预测生成互补的重采条带；（3）定制移动 App，用于执行采集任务，并支持自动化倾斜摄影与数据传输，无需额外机载硬件。

Result: 在真实航拍场景中，模型能有效识别可能重建不佳的区域；在 Expo West 实地实验中，针对性重采使额外视角的 PSNR 提升 10.95 dB。

Conclusion: OpenFlyScan 借助消费级无人机将采集、针对性重采与重建整合为一体，支持快速、低成本的城市资产创建。代码与模型将在 https://openflyscan.github.io/ 公开。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides high-fidelity scenes for large-scale embodied simulation, but constructing large-scale urban assets remains constrained by expensive equipment and delayed quality feedback. Preset surveys can leave complex surfaces insufficiently observed, with defects discovered only after reconstruction, requiring return visits and repeated processing. We present OpenFlyScan, a quality-guided aerial reconstruction system for consumer drones that integrates a GS quality model, a reacquisition planner, and a custom-designed mobile app. The model learns from GS rendering errors to predict regional reconstruction quality. Based on these predictions, the planner then generates complementary reacquisition strips to be executed through the app, which also supports automated oblique surveys and data transfer without additional hardware on board. Across real aerial scenes, the model effectively identifies regions that are likely to be poorly reconstructed. In the Expo West field experiment, targeted reacquisition improves PSNR at additional views by 10.95 dB. With consumer drones, OpenFlyScan integrates capture, targeted reacquisition, and reconstruction to support rapid, low-cost urban asset creation. Code and models will be made publicly available at https://openflyscan.github.io/.

</details>


### [139] [ME-Brain-1.0: Memory, Cognition and Action for Evolving Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24271)
*Wei He,Hengtao Li,Zhongrui Yu,Xuhan Zhu,Maokui He,Zide Liu,Xiyue Zhang,Xianwei Mao,Chunpeng Zhou,Jia Shi,Yanze Xin,Jingwen Li,Jingxie Zheng,Sijie Zeng,Chenfeng Wang,Fan Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Shuai Guo,Hengxuan Zhang,Pengfei Yu,Jia Shi,Yu Liu,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自演化具身系统 ME-Brain，通过“执行-经验获取-经验演化-改进执行”闭环，实现部署后无需重训即可持续进化，并在多个基准上显著超越现有最强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身系统依赖预训练能力且部署后固定不变，难以从物理交互中持续学习，限制了适应与进化能力。

Method: 围绕闭环组织三大模块：Evolvable Memory 将多模态轨迹整合为分级可复用经验；Cognitive Core 把物理经验转化为可迁移技能；Action Model 结合事件驱动关键帧、EventCell 局部世界预测与动作条件记忆调制，将计算聚焦于决策关键的时刻、区域与历史证据。

Result: Cognitive Core 在具身与智能体基准上分别领先最强对比模型 8.2 与 9.6 分；Action Model 在 RoboMME 达 47.88% 平均成功率（+3.26）；RoboDojo 达 21.51 平均分与 16.03% 成功率（超 π0.5 10.10 与 9.12 分）；ME-RealBench 六任务达 69.5 平均分与 66.7% 成功率（超 DM0.5 12.8 与 11.7 分）。

Conclusion: ME-Brain 将具身智能从“训练即冻结”转向“部署即演化”，在无需模型重训的情况下实现持续学习，并在多项基准上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Current embodied systems largely rely on pretrained capabilities that remain fixed after deployment, limiting their ability to learn from physical interaction. We introduce MachEmbodied-Brain (ME-Brain), a self-evolving embodied system organized around a closed loop of action execution, experience acquisition, experience evolution, and improved execution. Evolvable Memory consolidates multimodal trajectories into hierarchical, reusable experience; Cognitive Core transforms physical experience into transferable skills; and the Action Model combines event-driven keyframes, EventCell local-world prediction, and action-conditioned memory modulation to focus computation on decision-critical moments, regions, and historical evidence. Together, these modules shift embodied intelligence from train-and-freeze to deploy-and-evolve without model retraining. Cognitive Core outperforms the strongest comparison models by 8.2 and 9.6 points on embodied and agent benchmarks. The Action Model achieves 47.88% mean success on RoboMME, a 3.26-point improvement over the strongest baseline. On RoboDojo, it reaches a 21.51 mean Score and 16.03% success rate, exceeding $π_{0.5}$ by 10.10 and 9.12 points. On the six-task ME-RealBench, ME-Brain achieves a 69.5 mean Score and 66.7% success rate, outperforming DM0.5 by 12.8 and 11.7 points, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [vla.simd: Efficient CPU Inference for Language-Conditioned Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24274)
*Khanh D. Nguyen,Hoang M. Truong,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CPU 推理引擎 vla.simd 及基于 ACT 的语言条件策略 IMPACT，在无专用 GPU 条件下实现高效语言条件操作部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无专用 GPU 的设备上部署语言条件操作策略，需要高效推理以及能覆盖策略查询间延迟的动作块。论文关注查询延迟、执行时域与动作可用性之间的关系，并区分动作供给与反馈频率。

Method: vla.simd 结合共享 SIMD 微内核、可复用计算和目标特定优化；同时提出 IMPACT，一种基于 ACT 的策略，使用缓存文本表示和语言调制视觉特征。

Result: 在六种策略和四种 CPU 上，vla.simd 相比编译版 PyTorch 参考实现取得约 1.4 倍中位加速，并保持 fp32 数值保真度。IMPACT 是评估集中唯一在 Raspberry Pi 5 上提供至少 30 actions/s 的语言条件策略：90 秒热稳定后，fp32 下 33.5 actions/s，int8 下 81.2。GPU 评估在四个 LIBERO 套件上取得 76.4% 平均成功率（无机器人预训练）；指令打乱测试展示了对熟悉目标的选择能力。IMPACT 在 SO-101 机械臂、SmolVLA 在搭载 Robotiq 夹爪的 UR10e 上的试验，展示了两种机器人本体上的 CPU 部署。

Conclusion: vla.simd 与 IMPACT 表明，语言条件操作策略可在 CPU 上高效部署，在保持数值保真度的同时满足实时动作供给需求，并适用于多种机器人本体。

Abstract: Deploying language-conditioned manipulation without a dedicated GPU requires efficient inference and action chunks that cover the delay between policy queries. We present vla.simd, a CPU inference engine that combines shared SIMD micro-kernels, reusable computation, and target-specific optimization. We relate query latency and execution horizon to action availability under lagged and time-aligned execution, distinguishing action supply from feedback frequency. Across six policies and four CPUs, vla.simd achieves approximately $1.4\times$ median speedup over compiled PyTorch references while preserving fp32 numerical fidelity. We also introduce IMPACT, an ACT-based policy with cached text representations and language-modulated visual features. IMPACT is the only language-conditioned policy in our evaluated set that supplies at least 30 actions/s on the Raspberry Pi 5: after a 90 s thermal soak, it supplies 33.5 actions/s in fp32 and 81.2 with int8.
