Separate GPU evaluations yield $76.4\%$ mean success across four LIBERO suites without robot pretraining; instruction-shuffling tests demonstrate selection among familiar goals. Trials with IMPACT on an SO-101 arm and SmolVLA on a UR10e with a Robotiq gripper demonstrate CPU deployment on two robot embodiments.

</details>


### [141] [Performance-Preserving Online Adaptation in Social Navigation via Diffusion Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24317)
*Haruto Nagahisa,Kohei Matsumoto,Yuki Hyodo,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSRL方法，在社交导航部署环境中固定扩散策略、仅训练噪声策略进行微调，并集成多随机种子训练的扩散RL策略构建基础策略，实现保留原有性能的高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航中人与机器人交互复杂，深度强化学习被广泛研究，但仅靠仿真难以完整复现多样场景、机器人动力学及随部署环境变化的社会规范，因此在部署环境中微调很有前景；同时微调需保留基础模型性能，避免损害导航主目标（避让行人并到达目的地）。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的扩散引导（DSRL）：保持扩散策略固定，仅训练噪声策略，从而实现性能保持的学习；并集成用多个随机种子训练的基于扩散的强化学习策略来构建基础策略，以提升学习性能。

Result: 评估表明，与其他方法相比，所提方法可在保留性能的同时实现高效学习；并确认了对社会规范的适应带来的灵活行为控制能力，以及通过硬件在环仿真在实体机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: DSRL通过固定扩散策略、仅训练噪声策略并集成多种子基础策略，在社交导航部署环境中实现兼顾性能保持与高效学习，并具备适应社会规范和实体机器人部署的有效性。

Abstract: In social navigation, modeling the complex interactions between humans and robots is difficult, and deep reinforcement learning has therefore been actively studied. However, because simulation alone cannot fully reproduce diverse scenarios, robot dynamics, and the social conventions that vary across deployment environments, fine-tuning in the deployment environment is promising. In doing so, learning that preserves the base model's performance is required, so as not to compromise the primary objective of navigation, namely avoiding pedestrians and reaching the destination. In this study, we propose a method that applies diffusion steering via reinforcement learning (DSRL), which trains only the noise policy while keeping the diffusion policy fixed, thereby achieving learning that preserves performance. Furthermore, we integrate diffusion-based RL policies trained with multiple seeds to construct the base policy, improving learning performance. Our evaluation shows that, compared with other methods, the proposed method enables efficient learning while preserving performance, and we confirm flexible behavior control through adaptation to social conventions, as well as its effectiveness on a physical robot through hardware-in-the-loop simulation.

</details>


### [142] [LIBERO-VPro: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Visual Robustness of Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24350)
*Huiqiong Li,Zhiting Mei,Anirudha Majumdar,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-VPro 基准，系统评测机器人基础模型在闭环执行中的视觉鲁棒性，发现标称高性能掩盖了视觉 grounding 与适应的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型在标准操作基准上表现优异，但这些评测通常假设视觉观测干净、及时且一致，忽视了真实执行中视觉证据可能被扰动的情况，因此缺乏对闭环视觉鲁棒性的系统诊断手段。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-VPro 基准，通过扰动执行过程中可用的视觉证据来评测闭环视觉鲁棒性。涵盖四个维度：视觉证据退化、相机陈旧、视觉源一致性、任务相关场景变化，包含 12 类挑战、96 种实验设置、3,296 个任务条件用例。评测 3 个视觉-语言-动作模型和 3 个世界-动作模型，约 196,000 次仿真回合，并在 Franka Research 3 上进行 200 次真实世界 rollout。

Result: 强标称性能可掩盖视觉 grounding 与适应能力的重大弱点：模型在严重物体遮挡下仍常成功，但当局部交互线索被破坏或熟悉空间先验被违反时性能急剧下降；对陈旧或缺失观测高度敏感；当任务前提变化需要行为适应时表现困难；VLA 与 WAM 呈现不同的鲁棒性特征，表明视觉鲁棒性是多维且依赖架构的。

Conclusion: LIBERO-VPro 为开发能在挑战性视觉条件下更可靠地进行 grounding 与行为适应的机器人基础模型提供了系统性的诊断框架。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models achieve impressive performance on standard manipulation benchmarks, yet these evaluations typically assume clean, timely, and consistent visual observations throughout execution. We introduce LIBERO-VPro, a benchmark for systematically evaluating the closed-loop visual robustness of robotic foundation models by perturbing the visual evidence available during execution. LIBERO-VPro covers four complementary dimensions, including Visual Evidence Degradation, Camera Staleness, Visual Source Consistency, and Task-Relevant Scene Variation, spanning 12 challenge categories, 96 experimental settings, and 3,296 task-condition cases. We evaluate three vision-language-action models and three world-action models over approximately 196,000 simulated episodes, complemented by 200 real-world rollouts on a Franka Research 3. Our results reveal that strong nominal performance can mask substantial weaknesses in visual grounding and adaptation. Models often remain successful despite severe object-level occlusion, yet degrade sharply when local interaction cues are disrupted or familiar spatial priors are violated. They are also highly sensitive to stale or missing observations and struggle when changed task preconditions require behavioral adaptation. Finally, VLAs and WAMs exhibit distinct robustness profiles, showing that visual robustness is multi-dimensional and architecture-dependent. LIBERO-VPro provides a systematic diagnostic framework for developing robotic foundation models that can more reliably ground and adapt their actions under challenging visual conditions.

</details>


### [143] [Multi-Agent Transportation of Free-Flyers in Microgravity Via Pushing Interaction Under Human-in-the-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24376)
*Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Sihyun Cho,Youngkyoung Kong,Elias Krantz,Mani Hemanth Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向微重力环境下被动目标协同运输的安全关键框架，多个追踪机器人通过单侧推挤接触跟踪期望旋量并保证安全，采用CLF/CBF约束与混合整数推力分配实现延迟感知反馈控制，并通过Gazebo仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力下多个追踪机器人协同运输被动目标时，单侧推挤接触导致驱动稀疏且依赖构型，当期望推力分配变化时机器人需在目标表面物理移动，给稳定性与安全性带来挑战，需要兼顾参考跟踪与避障的控制框架。

Method: 构建延迟感知的反馈控制架构：在混合整数推力分配程序中引入控制李雅普诺夫函数（CLF）约束保证稳定性、控制障碍函数（CBF）约束保证安全性，从而在间歇性控制权限下实现推力分配与安全约束的统一求解。

Result: 框架能在控制权限间歇可用的条件下实现参考跟踪，并针对圆形障碍物保证避障安全，通过Gazebo仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为空间环境中自由飞行器的人机监督协同运输提供了基础框架，表明结合CLF/CBF与混合整数分配的延迟感知控制可在单侧推挤约束下同时保障跟踪性能与安全。

Abstract: We propose a safety-critical framework for the cooperative transportation of passive targets in microgravity, where a team of chaser robots acts through unilateral pushing contacts to track a human-provided desired twist while ensuring safe target motion. The pushing-only nature of the interaction introduces sparse, configuration-dependent actuation constraints requiring chasers to physically relocate on the target body when the desired pushing allocation changes. To address these challenges, we formulate a delay-aware feedback control architecture leveraging Control Lyapunov Function (CLF) and Control Barrier Function (CBF) constraints within a mixed-integer thrust allocation program to enforce stability and safety of the target, respectively. The proposed framework enables reference tracking while guaranteeing obstacle avoidance with a circular obstacle despite intermittent control authority, providing a foundation for human-supervised cooperative transportation of free-flyers in space environments. The proposed framework is validated through Gazebo simulations.

</details>


### [144] [Tactile-JEPA: Topology-Aware Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Distributed Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24385)
*Elizaveta Kovtun,Matvey Konovalov,Andrey Sakhovskiy,Semen Budennyy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tactile-JEPA：面向分布式电子皮肤的拓扑感知自监督预训练方法，利用传感器布局与连通图引导掩码预测，在力估计和手内姿态任务上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对机器人在视觉遮挡下的灵巧操作至关重要。然而，视觉编码器通常有预训练模型可用，触觉编码器却多从原始噪声信号从零训练，限制了其表达能力。现有触觉自监督学习主要针对视觉式触觉传感器，对分布式电子皮肤关注不足。电子皮肤的传感元件稀疏且不规则分布，直接套用视觉自监督方法效果不佳，因此需要专门针对其空间拓扑结构的预训练方法。

Method: 提出 Tactile-JEPA，一种高效的自监督预训练方法，利用触觉传感器的空间排列学习拓扑感知表征。模型以未掩码的传感元件预测被掩码元件的嵌入，并使用传感器连通图引导空间掩码策略。分析表明有效的触觉表征需同时捕捉局部接触细节与全局触觉表面状态，故采用双尺度掩码实现这一目标。

Result: 在涵盖磁性与压阻传感器、不同机器人本体、单传感器与双传感器配置的三个数据集上，Tactile-JEPA 相比此前最优方法将力估计误差降低 6.3%，手内姿态误差降低 20.8%，并在策略学习等其他下游任务中取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 触觉感知的收益关键取决于编码器预训练质量，而 Tactile-JEPA 直接针对该问题提出了有效解决方案，证明了针对电子皮肤拓扑结构设计的自监督预训练能显著提升下游触觉任务的性能。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is an essential modality for robots performing contact-rich, dexterous manipulation, particularly under visual occlusion. While pre-trained image encoders are standard in robot learning pipelines, tactile encoders are still commonly trained from scratch from raw, noisy signals, which might limit their expressivity. Existing self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches focus predominantly on vision-based tactile sensors, leaving distributed electronic skins largely unaddressed. These sensors, however, have a distinctive property: their sensing elements are sparse and irregularly arranged over the surface they cover, which makes direct reuse of visual SSL methods suboptimal. We present Tactile-JEPA, an efficient self-supervised pre-training method that uses the spatial arrangement of tactile sensors to learn topology-aware representations. Specifically, it is trained to predict the embeddings of masked sensing elements from the unmasked remainder, using the sensor connectivity graph to guide spatial masking. Our analysis shows that effective tactile representations require capturing both local contact details and the global state of the tactile surface, which we achieve through dual-scale masking. Across three diverse datasets spanning magnetic and piezoresistive sensors, different robot embodiments, and single- and paired-sensor configurations, Tactile-JEPA reduces force estimation error by 6.3% and in-hand orientation error by 20.8% over the prior state-of-the-art, with consistent gains in other downstream applications, including policy learning. Overall, our results demonstrate that the benefit of tactile sensing depends critically on the quality of encoder pre-training, a problem which Tactile-JEPA addresses directly. Code is available at https://github.com/E-Kovtun/tactile.

</details>


### [145] [Zeva-Ego: Egocentric Mid-Training with In-Context Causal Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24411)
*Bingjia Huang,Xin Ding,Fu Chen,Kun Li,Wei Sun,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Zeva-Ego统一框架，从第一人称人类视频中学习物理先验，并通过机器人交互持续进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频是可扩展的物理交互经验来源，但将其转化为机器人可执行知识并实现持续适应仍具挑战。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：以动作中心的编码器（ACE）将第一人称视觉转换转为动作中心监督用于VLA中期训练；上下文因果学习（ICCL）在部署时基于动作-效果反馈实现免参数适应。

Result: 将Ego数据扩展至1万小时，使RoboTwin成功率从63.8%提升至75.3%，匹配2000小时机器人演示（74.7%），数据比约4-5:1；ICCL在四次尝试内将成功率从58%提升至89%，无需参数更新。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一条从人类经验学习、并通过自身交互持续提升的可扩展具身智能路径。

Abstract: Egocentric video offers a scalable source of physical interaction experience, yet translating it into robot-executable knowledge and enabling continual adaptation remain challenging. We introduce Zeva-Ego, a unified framework that learns physical priors from human experience and evolves through robot interaction. An Action-Centric Encoder (ACE) converts egocentric visual transitions into action-centered supervision for VLA mid-training, while In-Context Causal Learning (ICCL) enables parameter-free adaptation from action-effect feedback at deployment. Scaling Ego data to 10K hours improves RoboTwin success from 63.8% to 75.3%, matching 2K hours of robot demonstrations (74.7%), corresponding to an empirical data ratio of roughly 4-5:1. With accumulated interaction experience, ICCL further improves success from 58% to 89% within four attempts without parameter updates. These results demonstrate a scalable path toward embodied intelligence that learns from human experience and continuously improves through its own interaction.

</details>


### [146] [Robotic Valve Turning: Axial Misalignment Correction Using Reaction Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24413)
*Amit Kumar,Sri Harsha Turlapati,Gautami Golani,Yang Lin,Ravi N. Banavar,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用反作用力矩校正机器人阀门操作中轴向错位的触觉更新控制律，理论证明一致渐近稳定，仿真与实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的错位估计易受标定误差、遮挡和接触几何不确定性影响；而反作用力矩直接来源于夹爪与阀门的物理交互，且与错位向量存在几何关系，因此更适合用于校正轴向错位。

Method: 利用错位向量与反作用力矩之间的几何关系，设计触觉更新控制律，并证明该控制律的一致渐近稳定性；随后通过仿真验证，并使用Kinova Gen3机械臂在不同阀门位置、初始错位范围-15°至15°下进行实验测试。

Result: 仿真验证了控制律的有效性；18个测试案例中，错位中位数的绝对值在2.46°以内，反作用力矩中位数绝对值在0.23Nm以内，表明方法具有较好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于反作用力矩的触觉控制律能够稳定且鲁棒地校正机器人阀门操作中的轴向错位，实验数据支持其实际有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a haptic update control law that uses reaction torques to correct axial misalignment during robotic valve manipulation. Unlike vision-based estimates, which can be affected by calibration errors, occlusion, and uncertainty in the contact geometry, reaction torques arise directly from the physical interaction between the gripper and valve. A geometric relationship exists between the error (misalignment) vector and these torques. The primary aim of this work is to propose a stable controller exploiting this geometric property. Our control law is proven to be uniformly asymptotically stable. Simulations are performed for verification. Furthermore, we experimentally test the robustness of our method using a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm for initial misalignments ranging from $-15^\circ$ to $15^\circ$ at 3 different valve positions and report the resulting data distribution. The absolute value of the median misalignment across all 18 test cases is found to be within $2.46^\circ$ and that of reaction torques within $0.23\mathrm{Nm}$.

</details>


### [147] [FoldQuantVLA: Native Low-Bit Quantization of Vision-Language-Action Models via Consistent Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24433)
*Hung T. Ho,Khanh D. Nguyen,Quang D. Nguyen,Thanh Q. Duong,Ngan Le,Meng Guo,Vien A. Ngo,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldQuantVLA是一个训练后量化框架，通过统一的激活表示与定制TensorRT插件，在语言主干与迭代动作专家中实现W4A4推理，并在多平台与真机上验证了速度与成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特视觉-语言-动作（VLA）推理需在降低观测到动作延迟的同时保持机器人行为；已有量化方法往往在激活表示上不一致，或需要策略再训练。

Method: 提出FoldQuantVLA训练后量化框架，将一致的激活表示贯穿校准、权值舍入与原生整数执行；结合通道缩放、块Hadamard变换与动态逐token量化，无需策略再训练；通过自定义TensorRT插件在Ada GPU与Jetson AGX Orin上以4比特权重和激活（W4A4）执行语言主干和迭代动作专家的投影；并保留部分语言注意力输出和FFN下投影为8比特（W8A8）。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv和两个机器人平台上评估，跨三个GR00T检查点及π0.5，W4A4相比浮点TensorRT在Orin上加速1.20–1.33倍，桌面端1.25–1.52倍；W8A8配置在四个检查点上均改善留出动作保真度；四个真实机器人任务中，GR00T N1.7成功率从均匀W4A4的80.0%提升至92.5%（每配置80次试验），Orin额外延迟仅1 ms。

Conclusion: 一致的激活表示与选择性高位保留可在不重训练的情况下实现高效低比特VLA推理，兼顾速度与真实机器人行为保真度。

Abstract: Low-bit vision-language-action inference must reduce observation-to-action latency while preserving robot behavior. We present FoldQuantVLA, a post-training quantization framework that carries a consistent activation representation through calibration, weight rounding, and native integer execution. It combines channel scaling and block Hadamard transforms with dynamic per-token quantization, without policy retraining. Custom TensorRT plugins execute projections in both the language backbone and iterative action expert with four-bit weights and activations (W4A4) on Ada GPUs and Jetson AGX Orin. Evaluation spans LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and two robot platforms. Across three GR00T checkpoints and $π_{0.5}$, W4A4 achieves $1.20$ to $1.33\times$ speedups over floating-point TensorRT on Orin and $1.25$ to $1.52\times$ on desktop. Retaining language attention-output and feed-forward down projections at eight bits (W8A8) improves held-out action fidelity on all four checkpoints. Across four real-robot tasks, this configuration raises observed GR00T N1.7 success from $80.0\%$ with uniform W4A4 to $92.5\%$ over 80 trials per configuration, with a measured additional Orin latency of 1 ms.

</details>


### [148] [ScaleMPA: Rethinking Scalable RRT* Acceleration With a Grid-Native Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24497)
*Zilong Wang,Yuzhou Chen,Xinyue He,Chen Zhang,Guanghui He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleMPA，一种基于网格化表示的RRT*运动规划加速器，通过多分辨率网格搜索引擎和哈希网格存储系统，在28nm CMOS上实现毫秒级规划延迟，相比SOTA加速器取得4.7×–44.4×的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模高维环境下的实时运动规划仍具挑战。先前的RRT*加速采用以树为中心的状态组织方式，虽降低了单次查询开销，但端到端复杂度仍为超线性，且结构性依赖限制了并行性。

Method: ScaleMPA以网格原生表示重构RRT*，用直接的基于网格的访问替代层次化遍历，从而缩短规划器关键路径并暴露细粒度并行性。为在稀疏高维规划中落地该重构，进一步提出多分辨率网格搜索引擎和哈希网格存储系统。基于28nm CMOS实现。

Result: ScaleMPA实现毫秒级规划延迟，相比当前最先进的运动规划加速器取得4.7×–44.4×的加速。

Conclusion: 通过网格化重构RRT*并结合多分辨率搜索与哈希存储，可有效降低关键路径、提升并行度，为大规模高维实时运动规划提供高效硬件加速方案。

Abstract: Real-time motion planning remains challenging in large and high-dimensional environments. Prior acceleration of RRT* follows tree-centric state organization, which reduces per-query cost but preserves superlinear end-to-end complexity and limits parallelism through structural dependencies. This paper presents ScaleMPA, a motion-planning accelerator that rethinks RRT* with a grid-native representation. By replacing hierarchical traversal with direct grid-based access, ScaleMPA reduces the planner critical path and exposes fine-grained parallelism. To make this reformulation practical under sparse high-dimensional planning, ScaleMPA further proposes a multi-resolution grid search engine and a hash-grid memory system. Implemented in 28 nm CMOS, ScaleMPA achieves millisecond-level planning latency and delivers 4.7$\times$--44.4$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art motion-planning accelerators.

</details>


### [149] [A Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft Acutuator Enabled by Single-Material 3D printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24499)
*Nan Huang,Lele Liu,Junfeng Lu,Yipan Zhu,Jiansheng Dai,Sicong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单体一体化力感知软体（MFPS）设计与制造方法，将非对称折纸弯曲（AOB）气腔与力感知软体（FPS）传感器通过单一材料一步FDM打印集成，实现了自感知软体驱动器并验证了抓取与力感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动本体感知驱动器可集成驱动与传感，但现有方案常因异质材料导致装配误差或应力集中，因此需要一种单体化、单材料的集成设计与制造方法。

Method: 基于导电热塑性聚氨酯（TPU）的应变电阻响应，设计并分析FPS传感器结构，开展传感特性的参数化分析；分析MFPS驱动器的FDM打印参数；完成驱动器制造并表征其驱动与本体感知性能。

Result: MFPS驱动器弯曲角达40°、输出力12.5 N；外力从0增至45 N时电阻变化26.9%；基于该驱动器研制的双指力感知夹爪，其抓取与力感知能力经实验验证有效。

Conclusion: MFPS单体单材料一体化设计方法为自感知驱动器的发展提供了新途径。

Abstract: Pneumatic proprioceptive actuators integrate actuation and sensing for soft robots that attract interest due to functional potential. Existing approaches often suffer from assembly errors or stress concentrations caused by heterogeneous materials. In this work, we propose the Monolithic Force-Proprioception Soft (MFPS) design and fabrication method that integrates an Asymmetric Origami Bending (AOB) chamber and a Force-Proprioception Soft (FPS) sensor with single material through one-step Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) fabrication. Based on the resistance response to strain of conductive thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU), we design and analyze the structure of the FPS sensor, and conduct parametric analysis on the sensing characteristics. The FDM fabrication parameters of the MFPS actuator are analyzed, followed by actuator fabrication and characterization of the actuation and proprioception performance. Experimental results show that the MFPS actuator achieves a bending angle of 40°, an output force of 12.5 N, and a resistance change of 26.9% as the applied external force increased from 0 to 45 N. A two-finger force-proprioception gripper is developed based on the MFPS actuator. The grasping and force-proprioception capabilities are experimentally validated, proving that the MFPS design method provides a new approach for the development of self-sensing actuators.

</details>


### [150] [TACIT: Tactile Contact Supervision for Spatial Attention in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24507)
*Yanhou Lai,Fucai Zhu,Ruiqiang Wang,Koichi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACIT 利用遥操作演示中测得的触觉接触来监督空间注意力，无需额外点标注，提升少样本视觉运动策略对物体位置变化的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本视觉运动策略能复现演示轨迹，但难以可靠跟踪物体位置变化；现有显式注意力方法依赖人工标注或视觉模型获取空间先验。

Method: 用触觉接触信息生成前置相机点云上的高斯目标，监督注意力头，其池化输出条件化视觉-触觉扩散策略；监督仅用于训练，推理时触觉观测仍作为输入。

Result: 每任务十次演示、五个放置区域的真实机器人基准：球放置成功率 66.7%、插销 73.3%，高于 3D 视觉-触觉融合（10.0%、20.0%）和纯视觉 DP3（20.0%、43.3%）。30 次试验均在 12 秒内进入 150 mm 接近区域，剩余失败均发生在到达之后。三个随机种子下优于输入匹配融合和无显式注意力监督的架构匹配对照。接触前与接触时监督无一致优劣。

Conclusion: 在评估工作空间内，测得的触觉接触能为少样本演示的接近行为提供有效空间监督。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained from a few demonstrations may reproduce demonstrated trajectories without reliably following changes in object position. Existing approaches with explicit attention typically obtain spatial priors from human annotation or visual models. We introduce TACIT (tactile contact informs attention), which uses measured tactile contacts from teleoperated demonstrations to supervise spatial attention without additional point annotation. Gaussian targets over preceding camera point clouds supervise an attention head whose pooled output conditions a visuotactile diffusion policy. Targets are used only during training; tactile observations remain inputs at inference. In the primary real-robot benchmark, with ten demonstrations per task and five demonstrated placement regions, TACIT achieves 66.7% success on ball placement and 73.3% on peg insertion, compared with 10.0% and 20.0% for input-matched 3D visuotactile fusion and 20.0% and 43.3% for vision-only DP3. TACIT enters the 150 mm palm-to-object approach region within 12 seconds in all 30 trials per task; all remaining failures occur after arrival. Across three training seeds on real ball and simulated peg, TACIT outperforms input-matched fusion and an architecture-matched control without explicit attention supervision, supporting the contribution of supervision beyond branch capacity. Pre-contact and contact-time supervision show no consistent ordering. These results demonstrate that measured tactile contact provides effective spatial supervision for approach behavior from few demonstrations within the evaluated workspace.

</details>


### [151] [InsertAnything: Generalizable Contact-Rich Precision Insertion from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24511)
*Zhenghua Ma,Xinpan Meng,Zeyu Liu,Muyuan Ma,Hengdi Zhang,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全在仿真中训练的强化学习框架，无需真实演示或微调，即可直接部署机器人精密插入策略，实现对0.02 mm间隙零件的自主插入与跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集的精密插入是机器人装配的关键技能。小间隙使插入对对准误差敏感，易发生碰撞与卡滞；零件几何与间隙的变化又使策略难以复用。作者希望摆脱真实演示与策略微调，仅靠仿真训练获得可直接部署的精密插入能力。

Method: 在仿真中训练强化学习插入策略：将目标位姿与紧凑的三维指尖力反馈相结合，使策略在孔位估计存在误差时仍能搜索对准并修正运动；采用解耦的门控奖励协调对准与插入两个阶段；通过力信号平滑与状态无关的标准差稳定训练过程。

Result: 在多种孔几何、最小标称间隙0.02 mm的真实插入任务中成功完成任务，在孔位误差下提升成功率并降低峰值接触力；跨间隙与跨几何评估验证了策略泛化性。在ManipulationNet的peg-in-hole基准的Human-in-the-Loop协议下取得首个满分20/20，且插入动作完全自主；仅在仿真六边形插入任务上训练的单策略在八个未见真实插入任务上取得95.0%的总成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，完全在仿真中学习即可获得可直接部署并跨真实任务复用的精密插入技能。项目网站提供开源的仿真与真机实验脚本、资产及训练好的检查点。

Abstract: Contact-rich precision insertion is a key manipulation skill in robotic assembly. Tight clearances make insertion more sensitive to alignment errors and prone to collisions and jamming, while variations in geometry and clearance across parts further complicate policy reuse. We present a reinforcement learning framework that trains insertion policies entirely in simulation for direct deployment without real-world demonstrations or policy fine-tuning. By combining target poses with compact three-dimensional fingertip force feedback, the policy learns to search for alignment and correct its motion despite errors in the estimated hole position. A decoupled gated reward coordinates alignment and insertion. Force-signal smoothing and state-independent standard deviations stabilize the learning process. The resulting policies perform real-world insertion across multiple hole geometries with a minimum nominal clearance of 0.02 mm and improve success while reducing peak contact forces under hole-position errors. Cross-clearance and cross-geometry evaluations further confirm policy generalization. The system achieved the first perfect score of 20/20 on ManipulationNet's peg-in-hole benchmark under its Human-in-the-Loop protocol, with fully autonomous insertion motions. A single policy trained only on a simulated hexagonal insertion task achieved an overall success rate of 95.0% across eight unseen real-world insertion tasks. These results show that learning entirely in simulation can yield precision insertion skills that can be deployed directly and reused across real-world tasks. The project website (https://mzhsoul.github.io/InsertAnything/) provides open-source simulation and real-robot experiment scripts, assets, and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [152] [Bridge3D: Enabling Vision-Language-Action Models to See and Act in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24525)
*Haoxuan Li,Sixu Yan,Lianghui Zhu,Xuanlai Tang,Shikang Wang,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge3D 将隐式与显式 3D 几何引导融入预训练 2D VLA 模型，使其具备 3D“看”与“动”的能力，在仿真与真机任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要基于 2D 观测训练，天然限制其精细空间操作能力；此前方法虽引入隐式空间先验，但缺乏显式几何引导。

Method: 提出 Bridge3D，包含两种策略：（1）隐式融合，用 3D 基础模型特征增强视觉 token，提升 3D“感知”；（2）显式条件化，将动作去噪与显式 3D 语义场结合，实现 3D“行动”。同时采用逐层线性探测提升学习效率。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 基准上比 π₀ 高出 14.0 个百分点；真机实验中比 Spatial Forcing 高出 11.7 个百分点，体现出在高精度、空间敏感操作任务上的优势。

Conclusion: 融合隐式与显式 3D 几何引导可有效增强 2D VLA 模型的 3D 空间操作能力，Bridge3D 在高精度空间敏感任务中表现优越。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable generalization in robotic manipulation via large-scale multimodal pretraining. However, VLA models are mainly trained on 2D-centric observations, which inherently constrains their capacity for precise spatial manipulation. Previous methods enhance 3D awareness by introducing implicit spatial priors, but still lack explicit geometry guidance. In this paper, we propose Bridge3D that integrates both implicit and explicit 3D geometry guidance into pre-trained 2D VLA models, enabling them to ''see'' and ''act'' in 3D. Bridge3D introduces two strategies: 1) Implicit Fusion, which enriches visual tokens with features from 3D foundation models to improve ''seeing'' in 3D; 2) Explicit Conditioning, which integrates action denoising with an explicit 3D semantic field to achieve ''acting'' in 3D. Furthermore, we utilize the proposed layer-wise linear probing to improve learning efficiency. Experiments show that Bridge3D achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art methods. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, Bridge3D exceeds $π_0$ by 14.0 percentage points, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms Spatial Forcing by 11.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate Bridge3D's strong capabilities in high-precision and spatial-sensitive manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Estimation and Control of Tensegrity Manipulator Kinematics based on Strut Inclination Angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24535)
*Tufail Ahmad Bhat,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种降阶模型，用于张拉整体（tensegrity）连续机械臂的形状与姿态控制，并首次在实物上实现基于IMU的实时形状估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性连杆机器人由离散关节定义，而连续体机器人（continuum robots）具有复杂的连续弯曲构型，难以表达以用于闭环控制。已有建模方法多假设连续弹性骨架，不适用于由刚性杆与张紧绳索组成的张拉整体机械臂，该领域基本处于空白。

Method: 将机械臂建模为串联并联连杆机构，构建一个优化问题，结合张拉整体结构的几何约束与嵌入杆件中的IMU传感器信息，实现降阶的形状与姿态估计，并用简单PI控制器进行姿态控制。

Result: 所提方法能够从任意静态构型估计单模块张拉整体结构及多模块张拉整体机械臂的形状，并通过PI控制器实现期望姿态。

Conclusion: 这是首个在完整尺寸张拉整体机械臂上进行实时IMU形状估计的实验演示，验证了降阶模型在张拉整体连续机械臂形状估计与姿态控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Unlike conventional rigid-link robots defined by discrete joints, continuum robots pose a fundamental challenge for expressing their complex continuous bending configurations for closed-loop control. Several modelling approaches have been proposed for conventional continuum robots, but tensegrity-based continuum robots remain largely open. Moreover, many of these approaches assume a continuous elastic backbone and are therefore not directly applicable to tensegrity manipulators, whose bodies are networks of rigid struts and tensioned cables. This work presents a reduced-order model for shape and posture control of a tensegrity-based continuum manipulator. The manipulator is modelled as a serially connected parallel-link mechanism. The proposed method is formulated as an optimization problem that uses geometric constraints of the tensegrity structure together with information from the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors embedded in the strut elements. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first experimental demonstration of a real-time IMU-based shape estimation method on a full-scale tensegrity manipulator and demonstrates posture control using a simple Proportional-Integral (PI) controller. The results show that the proposed method can estimate the shape of both single-module tensegrity structures and multi-module tensegrity manipulators from arbitrary static configurations and achieve desired postures.

</details>


### [154] [MIRA: Real-Time Full-Duplex Human-Robot Interaction for Embodied Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24547)
*Lijian Lin,Ye Zhu,Fan Zhang,Yunfei Liu,Baofeng Li,Xianwen Zeng,Jianan Wang,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRA是一个面向全双工具身陪伴交互的统一框架，能够从流式语音中推断用户意图，同步生成回应文本、韵律时序与可中断的机器人动作，并在Astribot S1人形机器人上实现了实时部署与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时具身陪伴交互要求机器人从流式语音中推断用户意图、及时生成回应并执行富有表现力且可中断的动作。然而现有系统通常将对话编排与手势合成解耦，依赖完整音频进行离线动作生成，难以解决在增量输入和不确定轮次边界下，如何动态同步回应内容、韵律时序与物理安全的问题。

Method: MIRA以流式用户语音、对话历史与语音情感为输入，同时预测回应文本和显式的具身线索。离散社交行为（如倾听、问候）映射到已验证的机器人轨迹，开放式说话则配对流式共语动作。生成动作由"预测多于提交"的滑动窗口控制，提供时序前瞻以保证动作连续性，同时将物理承诺限制在可取消的短前缀内。此外设计了CORTEX双时间尺度交互策略，兼顾低延迟流式处理与深思熟虑的轮次决策，并由机器人侧执行层以控制频率强制物理安全约束。

Result: 在Astribot S1人形机器人上部署MIRA。定量评估表明，其音频-动作对齐性能与当前最先进的运动生成基线相比具有竞争力，同时真机部署测量刻画了流式响应能力与中断处理表现。

Conclusion: MIRA通过统一框架将对话编排、动作生成与安全执行整合，实现了流式、可中断、可安全部署的具身陪伴交互，验证了全双工具身交互在真实机器人上的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time embodied companion interaction requires a robot to infer user intent from streaming speech, generate timely responses, and execute expressive, interruptible motions. Existing systems typically decouple dialogue orchestration from gesture synthesis, relying on offline motion generation from complete audio. This separation leaves open how a deployed robot can dynamically synchronize response content, prosodic timing, and physical safety under incremental inputs and uncertain turn boundaries. We present MIRA, a unified framework for full-duplex embodied companion interaction. Given streaming user speech, dialogue history, and vocal affect, MIRA predicts both the response text and an explicit embodiment cue. Discrete social behaviors (\eg listening, greeting) are mapped to validated robot trajectories, while open-ended speaking is paired with streaming, co-speech motion. This generative motion is governed by a predict-more-than-commit sliding window that provides temporal look-ahead for motion continuity while limiting physical commitment to a short, cancellable prefix. Crucially, we design CORTEX, a dual-timescale interaction policy that manages low-latency streaming and deliberative turn decisions, backed by a robot-side execution layer that enforces physical safety constraints at the control rate. We deploy MIRA on an Astribot S1 humanoid robot. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate competitive audio-motion alignment relative to state-of-the-art motion-generation baselines, while real-robot deployment measurements characterize streaming responsiveness and interruption handling.

</details>


### [155] [Smoothness as a Constraint for Stable Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24552)
*Utsav Panchal,Denis Kleyko,Unal Artan,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeCap，一种解耦约束感知的约束强化学习算法，将人形机器人全身平滑性拆分为上半身与下半身两个约束组，以显式物理运动限制实现平滑控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界人形机器人需要既响应任务又物理平滑的全身控制策略，但平滑需求在身体上并不统一：下半身需保持足够反应性，上半身需严格约束以维持稳定。现有强化学习方法多通过奖励中的辅助项施加平滑，与任务目标相互竞争，把身体视为均质，且无法直接控制决定平滑行为的物理量。

Method: DeCap是一种约束强化学习算法，将全身平滑性解耦为上半身和下半身两个独立的约束组，每组将平滑性表述为对物理运动极限的显式约束；为在可行域边界附近提升约束满足率，引入有界障碍惩罚，在接近极限时主动激活且在约束极限处保持有界。

Result: 在真实世界人形机器人全身控制任务上，相比基于奖励的平滑策略，DeCap将上半身动作速率降低2.50倍、加速度降低2.18倍，同时改善了下半身平滑性并减少瞬态运动。

Conclusion: 一组固定的平滑约束可跨多种地形迁移，减少大量奖励调参需求，验证了解耦约束方法在真实人形控制中的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, particularly humanoid robots deployed in real world scenarios require whole-body control policies that are both task-responsive and physically smooth. However, smoothness is not uniform across the body: lower body must remain sufficiently reactive, while the upper body must be tightly regulated to preserve stability. Existing reinforcement learning approaches typically impose smoothness through auxiliary terms in the reward function, which compete with task objectives, treating the body as uniform and provide no direct control over the physical quantities responsible for smooth behavior. We introduce DeCap (Decoupled Constraint-aware policy), a constrained reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples whole-body smoothness into separate upper- and lower-body constraint groups, each formulates smoothness as explicit constraints on physical motion limits. To improve constraint satisfaction near feasibility boundaries, DeCap incorporates a bounded barrier penalty that activates proactively as limits are approached and remains bounded at the constraint limit. On real-world humanoid whole-body control task, DeCap reduces upper-body action rate by 2.50x and acceleration by 2.18x relative to reward-based smoothness policies, while also improving lower-body smoothness and reducing transient motion. We demonstrate that a fixed set of smoothness constraints transfers across diverse terrains, alleviating the need of extensive reward tuning.

</details>


### [156] [ARSTAG: An Agentic Real2Sim2Real System for Task-Specific Robot Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24563)
*Bowei Li,Yuner Zhang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARSTAG 是一个智能体式 Real2Sim2Real 系统，可将单张 RGB 图像和自然语言指令直接转化为机器人策略学习数据，实现跨七种操作任务的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉运动策略适应到新操作任务通常需要大量人工工程或遥操作数据采集；仿真虽能规模化生成任务数据，但场景构建、专家行为设计与数据生成配置仍需逐任务投入大量精力。

Method: 提出 ARSTAG，一个智能体式 Real2Sim2Real 系统。采用分层语言智能体：构建任务范围的仿真场景、生成机器人可行的演示，并通过任务一致的随机化扩展训练分布；由协调智能体管理跨阶段反馈与恢复。

Result: 在涵盖抓取、放置与堆叠的七种操作任务上，ARSTAG 生成的演示使三种视觉运动策略架构成功迁移至双臂机器人，其中 pi0.5 取得平均 74.6% 的真实世界成功率。消融实验表明任务一致随机化显著提升鲁棒性，且策略性能随生成数据集规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: ARSTAG 能够仅凭单张图像与自然语言指令自动化生成仿真数据，无需逐任务人工工程，有效支撑仿真到现实的策略迁移，且数据规模与随机化设计对性能至关重要。

Abstract: Adapting visuomotor policies to new manipulation tasks often requires substantial manual engineering or teleoperated data collection. Simulation can provide task-specific data at scale, but constructing the scene, designing expert behavior, and configuring data generation still require significant per-task effort. We present ARSTAG, an agentic Real2Sim2Real system that turns a single RGB image and a natural-language instruction directly into robot policy-learning data. A hierarchy of language agents constructs a task-scoped simulation scene, generates robot-feasible demonstrations, and expands the training distribution through task-consistent randomization, while a coordinator agent manages cross-stage feedback and recovery. Across seven manipulation tasks spanning grasping, placement, and stacking, the ARSTAG-generated demonstrations enable sim-to-real transfer of three visuomotor policy architectures to a dual-arm robot, with pi0.5 achieving an average real-world success rate of 74.6%. Ablations show that task-consistent randomization substantially improves robustness, and policy performance increases with generated dataset size. Project webpage: https://boweili666.github.io/ARSTAG/.

</details>


### [157] [What do VLM-Based Vision-Language Navigation Models Rely on: Interpreting and Steering Policy Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24576)
*Débora Oliveira Makowski,Samiran Gode,Abhijeet Nayak,Marco Hutter,Cordelia Schmid,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言导航(VLN)模型的可解释性与可操控性，通过干预式指标衡量视觉观测、指令与视觉记忆对导航决策的因果影响，发现模型对多模态输入均敏感，并能编码导航进度、保留VLM骨干的语义结构，可实现概念级操控及零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLN模型依赖预训练大型视觉语言模型预测导航动作，虽能进行多模态推理，但信息如何在模态间路由、什么机制驱动导航决策并不清楚，因此难以判断模型是否基于相关语义线索、能否追踪任务进度。

Method: 采用基于干预的指标，测量视觉观测、指令和视觉记忆对导航决策的因果影响；分析模型内部激活所编码的导航进度与语义结构；提取抽象行为的激活向量并进行概念级操控。

Result: 导航策略对所有输入模态均敏感，不依赖单一模态；模型编码了导航进度并保留VLM骨干的语义结构；通过内部激活可实现概念级操控；将抽象行为的激活向量零样本迁移到分布外真实场景可提升性能，无需额外微调。

Conclusion: VLN模型具有可解释性与可操控性，其决策基于多模态语义线索并能追踪任务进度，内部激活可用于概念级操控与零样本行为迁移。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) models rely mostly on pre-trained large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to predict navigation actions. While this fusion of language instructions and visual observations allows multimodal reasoning, it obscures how information is routed across modalities or what mechanisms drive navigation decisions. Thus, it remains unclear whether VLN models ground their predictions in relevant semantic cues or can track task progress. In this work, we study the interpretability and steerability of VLN models. We use intervention-based metrics that measure how visual observations, instructions, and visual memory causally influence navigation decisions. Our results show that these navigation policies are sensitive to all input modalities and do not depend on a single one. We further show that these agents encode navigation progress and retain semantic structure from their VLM backbones, enabling concept-level steering through internal activations. Finally, we extract activation vectors for abstract behaviors to transfer them zero-shot to out-of-distribution real-world scenarios, improving performance without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [158] [Learning tactile perception from high-bandwidth single-point sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24621)
*Joseph Rigal,Emmanuel Virot,Caroline Pascal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpectRobot 将单点触觉信号转换为时频谱图，用视觉编码器处理，在稀疏测量下实现高带宽触觉感知，用于遮挡下的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的机器人操作多依赖空间分布式的触觉传感器（如触觉阵列），通过提高空间密度来获取信息。但这类方案成本高、接触面易磨损，且传统摄像头无法提供接触动力学中的时间与频率信息。因此作者希望探索利用稀疏、高带宽的单点测量所蕴含的丰富动态信息，作为提升空间密度的替代或补充。

Method: 提出 SpectRobot 框架：把单点触觉信号转成紧凑的时频谱图，将高带宽触觉历史编码为固定尺寸的图像式表示，可直接用标准视觉编码器和原本为视觉设计的学习流程处理，同时保留摄像头无法获取的时间与频率信息。传感器安装在远离接触面但与之机械耦合的位置，减少磨损、提升恶劣环境和长期部署的鲁棒性。实验覆盖加速度、力、应变等不同触觉感知技术。

Result: (1) 机器人可利用单点振动信号完成视觉被遮挡的操作任务；(2) 时间历史对策略性能影响显著，感知带宽决定可用的频谱信息，测量频率可达 100 kHz；(3) 同一表示可跨基于加速度、力或应变的多种触觉传感技术使用；此外，原本依赖研究级仪器的能力可用现成商用硬件实现。

Conclusion: 高带宽触觉传感的更广泛可及性有助于将接触动力学融入具身学习系统，并在部分任务上为增加触觉空间密度提供替代或补充方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is increasingly being incorporated into learning-based robotic manipulation, yet many existing approaches rely on spatially distributed sensors. Here we introduce SpectRobot, a framework that transforms single-point tactile signals into compact time-frequency spectrograms. These spectrograms encode high-bandwidth tactile histories as fixed-size image-like representations. They can be processed by standard vision encoders and integrated into learning pipelines originally developed for vision, while preserving temporal and frequency information unavailable to conventional cameras.
  Rather than increasing spatial density through arrays of tactile elements, SpectRobot exploits the rich dynamics contained in sparse, high-bandwidth single-point measurements. In our implementation, the sensors are mounted away from the contact surface while remaining mechanically coupled to it, reducing direct exposure to wear and potentially improving robustness in harsh environments and for long-term deployment on dexterous robots.
  Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) a robot can exploit single-point vibration signals to solve a visually occluded manipulation task; (2) temporal history strongly influences policy performance, while sensing bandwidth controls the spectral information available, with measurements extending to 100~kHz; and (3) the same representation can be used across different tactile sensing technologies mediated by acceleration, force, or strain. We further show that capabilities previously associated with research-grade instrumentation can be accessed using readily available, off-the-shelf hardware. We believe that broader access to high-bandwidth tactile sensing could facilitate the integration of contact dynamics into embodied learning systems and, for some tasks, offer an alternative or complement to increasing the spatial density of tactile sensing.

</details>


### [159] [From Semantic Decisions to Feasible Trajectories: Self-Evolving LLM-Guided Optimal Control for Narrow-Space Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24631)
*Zhengbao Yao,Yuanfu Luo,Kehan Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SE-LLM-OCP框架，用LLM做高层离散机动决策，用最优控制模块保证底层动力学与碰撞约束，并通过在线失败反馈重规划与离线知识库自演化，在狭窄非凸场景实现更安全的自主泊车并跨平台迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸狭窄环境下的自主泊车仍具挑战：最优控制方法虽能显式满足动力学与碰撞约束，但非凸性损害求解器鲁棒性、易导致失败；LLM具备语义推理能力，但直接生成稠密轨迹难以保证物理可行性。因此需要结合二者优势。

Method: SE-LLM-OCP统一框架：在线时，LLM提出稀疏机动计划，将泊车任务分解为一系列短时域轨迹优化问题，由底层求解器顺序求解最优控制问题；若求解失败，LLM汇总来自求解器与验证阶段的失败证据以指导重规划。离线时，框架由累积的在线失败驱动，从零自动演化出结构化决策知识库。

Result: 在类车车辆模型与差速驱动机器人的仿真中验证：SE-LLM-OCP能在狭窄场景实现更安全的自主泊车，并证明同一机动表示可迁移到不同运动学平台。

Conclusion: 将LLM高层离散决策与最优控制底层约束执行相结合，并借助失败驱动的在线重规划与离线知识演化，可有效缓解非凸求解失败问题，提升狭窄环境泊车的安全性与跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous parking in nonconvex and narrow environments remains challenging. Although optimal-control methods can explicitly enforce vehicle dynamics and collision constraints, nonconvexity compromises solver robustness and can cause failures. Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic reasoning capabilities, but directly generating dense trajectories makes it difficult to guarantee physical feasibility. We introduce SE-LLM-OCP, a unified framework in which LLMs make high-level discrete maneuver decisions, while an optimal-control module enforces low-level vehicle dynamics and collision constraints. Online, the LLM proposes sparse maneuver plans, decomposing the parking task into a sequence of short-horizon trajectory-optimization problems. A low-level solver then sequentially solves optimal-control problems. If the solver fails, the LLM aggregates failure evidence from the solver and validation stages to guide replanning. Offline, SE-LLM-OCP automatically evolves a structured decision-making knowledge base from scratch, driven by accumulated online failures. We validate our proposed framework in simulation on a car-like vehicle model and on a differential-drive robot. Our experimental results show that SE-LLM-OCP enables safer autonomous parking in narrow scenarios and demonstrates transfer of the same maneuver representation to a different kinematic platform.

</details>


### [160] [Feasibility Distance Fields for Heterogeneous Constraints in Robot Configuration Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24632)
*Xijing Cui,Huayan Pu,Jun Luo,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性距离场（FDF），用统一关节空间度量把异构约束统一为可比的配置空间裕度，并用神经网络近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人约束监测指标单位与梯度尺度不可比，无法给出统一的"距违规还剩多少构型空间运动"度量，因此难以在异构约束间组合与权衡。

Method: 定义FDF为固定正定关节空间度量下到不可行配置集并集的距离；借助距离集理论获得1-Lipschitz、几乎处处可微、单位对偶梯度范数；做可容许性分析，为外部/自碰撞、关节限位、灵巧度、笛卡尔与任务投影柔顺性、负载下关节力矩、动态可操作度给出可容许表述；用逐点最小值组合约束；生成投影标签并以距离损失加Eikonal惩罚训练神经近似。

Result: 在UR5e与双臂单元上评估七个场；8000个构型下最大可行侧割线比0.920，学习梯度范数均值0.994–0.998，投影残差0.011–0.034 rad；24条随机障碍路径中外部与组合碰撞场3 cm内成功率90.4%与91.6%，符号错误率低于2%。

Conclusion: FDF为异构约束提供统一配置空间裕度，多约束可经同一度量组合；误差主要出现在中轴附近与稀疏采样边界。

Abstract: Robot manipulators are monitored by constraint-specific indicators whose units and gradient scales are not comparable, so they do not provide a common measure of the configuration-space motion remaining before violation. We define the feasibility distance field (FDF) as the distance, under a fixed positive-definite joint-space metric, to the union of infeasible configuration sets. Classical distance-to-set theory gives 1-Lipschitz continuity, almost-everywhere differentiability, and unit dual-gradient norm wherever the nearest projection is unique. The robotics contribution is an admissibility analysis showing when practical constraints define non-empty closed sets. We derive admissible formulations for external and self-collision, joint limits, dexterity, Cartesian and task-projected compliance, joint torque under payload, and dynamic manipulability. Since every field uses the same metric, heterogeneous constraints compose by a pointwise minimum, conditioned constraints retain a fixed distance space, and multi-robot constraints produce block-sparse gradients that identify which robots must react. We generate projection-based labels and train neural approximations with a distance loss and an Eikonal penalty. Simulations on a UR5e and a dual-arm cell evaluate seven fields using value, projection, sign, gradient, composition, and moving-obstacle diagnostics. Across 8,000 configurations, the largest feasible-side secant ratio is 0.920, mean learned gradient norms range from 0.994 to 0.998, and projection residuals range from 0.011 to 0.034 rad. Across 24 random obstacle paths, the external and composed collision fields achieve 90.4% and 91.6% success within 3 cm, with sign-error rates below 2%. The results support a common configuration-space margin and identify approximation errors near medial axes and sparsely sampled boundaries.

</details>


### [161] [Touch2Robot: Robot Touch in the Human Demonstration Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24660)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiaoyang Cheng,Hong Ying,Xiaoying Zhou,Jiaming Jiang,Haoran Guo,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Robot 让人类在采集示教时实时看到目标机器人手的接触，从而提升灵巧操作数据采集的质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示教可规模化采集操作数据，但人手的接触迁移到机器人手时可能不稳定或不可行；直接在目标机器人上采集虽避免失配，却大幅增加成本。该工作旨在平衡二者权衡。

Method: 采集人手运动、触觉手套测量与物体运动，用这些记录引导物体专属 RL 策略复现物体运动并偏好与人手触觉一致的接触；将学习到的行为蒸馏为统一实时重定向器，把人类观测与物体几何映射为机器人手构型；采集时将预测构型与仿真中物体位姿同步，重建机器人与物体接触并可视化，帮助示教者调整后续交互。

Result: 在四个真实任务中，平均真机重放完成率从 37.9% 提升到 72.1%（相对纯视觉反馈）；每次重放成功示教的采集时间从 58.6 秒降至 18.2 秒；重建的目标手接触对真实机器人触觉测量达到 44.2% F1；基于 Touch2Robot 示教训练的策略在下游 Diffusion Policy 上比纯视觉反馈高 29.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将机器人触觉引入人类示教回路，能同时提升可扩展灵巧数据采集的质量与效率。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer a scalable way to collect manipulation data, but their contacts may be unstable or infeasible when transferred to a robot hand. Collecting demonstrations directly on the target robot avoids this mismatch, but substantially increases the cost of data collection. To address this trade-off, we present \textbf{Touch2Robot}, a framework that lets humans collect demonstrations while seeing how the target robot hand would contact the object. We capture human hand motion, tactile-glove measurements, and object motion during human manipulation. These recordings guide object-specific RL policies to reproduce the demonstrated object motion while favoring contacts consistent with the recorded human touch. We distill the learned behaviors into a unified real-time retargeter that maps incoming human observations and object geometry to robot hand configurations. During collection, the predicted robot configuration is synchronized with the tracked object pose in simulation to reconstruct robot-object contacts, which are visualized to help the demonstrator adapt subsequent interactions to the target hand. Across four real-world tasks, Touch2Robot improves average real-robot replay completion from 37.9\% to 72.1\% over visual-only feedback, while reducing the collection time per replay-successful demonstration from 58.6~s to 18.2~s. Reconstructed target-hand contacts achieve 44.2\% F1 against real-robot tactile measurements, and policies trained on Touch2Robot demonstrations improve downstream Diffusion Policy performance by 29.1 percentage points over visual-only feedback. These results show that bringing robot touch into the human demonstration loop improves both the quality and efficiency of scalable dexterous data collection. \textit{Project webpage: \href{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}{https://Touch2Robot.github.io/}.}

</details>


### [162] [Think Like a World Model, Act Like a VLA: Distilling World-Model Representations into Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24682)
*Trung Dao,Sankalp Yamsani,Jaden Park,Joohyung Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将世界模型的"物理接地"能力与"未来生成"计算解耦，通过一次性缓存的冻结世界模型特征做对齐蒸馏，使普通VLA策略在零额外推理开销下获得鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型直接映射观测到动作，缺乏对世界响应方式的约束，鲁棒性受数据覆盖范围限制；而世界模型虽具备这种接地能力，但逐帧滚动预测需数秒每决策，无法进入控制回路。作者希望在不牺牲推理效率的前提下继承世界模型的表征接地性。

Method: 在常规VLA训练中仅增加一个特征对齐项：先用冻结的世界模型对训练帧一次性推理并缓存其特征，学生策略学习与该缓存对齐。训练时无需加载教师模型，投影器在训练后丢弃，部署策略与未蒸馏基线完全一致（32ms、1.86GB，消费级RTX 5090）。该目标在0.8B学生模型上验证，并做了学生规模、骨干、对齐层与教师模型的消融。

Result: 0.8B学生在LIBERO上达97.9%；RoboCasa-GR1人形操作从48.2%提升至50.5%；同一目标可迁移到真机，覆盖单臂与双臂平台。增益在更换学生规模、骨干、对齐层与教师后依然成立。

Conclusion: 世界模型的知识存在于内部特征中，生成未来只是产生这些特征的目标，因此可只继承接地性而舍弃生成机制。所有增益归因于表征而非容量或测试时算力增加，说明这是一种广泛可迁移的表征先验，而非两个特定网络间的脆弱对齐。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map observations to actions with no objective that accounts for how the world responds, so their robustness is bounded primarily by data coverage. World models carry precisely that missing objective and are better grounded for it, yet rolling the future forward costs seconds per decision and rules them out of the control loop. We show the two can be separated. What a world model knows about physical scenes lives in its \emph{internal features}; generating the future is merely the objective that produced them, so the grounding can be inherited while the generative machinery is left behind. We add one feature-alignment term to ordinary VLA training: a frozen world model is run over the training frames once and cached, and the student learns to agree with that cache. No teacher is loaded during training, the projector is discarded after it, and the deployed policy is identical to the undistilled baseline, running in $32$~ms and $1.86$~GB on a consumer RTX~5090, so every gain is attributable to the representation rather than to added capacity or test-time compute. A $0.8$B student reaches $97.9\%$ on LIBERO, improves from $48.2\%$ to $50.5\%$ on RoboCasa-GR1 humanoid manipulation, and the same objective carries over to real hardware, on both a single-arm and a bimanual platform. The gain survives changes of student scale, backbone, alignment layer, and teacher, indicating a broad representational prior rather than a fragile alignment between two particular networks. Project page: https://thaw-vla.trung-dt.com/.

</details>


### [163] [AC-DC: Adaptive Communication for Scalable Dynamic Average Consensus in Multi-Robot Ergodic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24702)
*Robin Inho Kee,Begum Cannataro,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AC-DC方法，用于多机器人系统在有限通信条件下的可扩展动态平均一致性，并应用于动态优先级遍历搜索，显著降低通信量与不确定性协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在有限距离、有限速率和干扰受限通信下实现可扩展的分布式动态平均一致性（DC）存在挑战，尤其是需要在局部输入不断变化时保持共识状态的准确估计。

Method: 提出Adaptive Communication for Dynamic Average Consensus (AC-DC)，联合自适应决定谁与谁通信、何时通信以及交换共识状态的哪些部分，依据局部输入和成功接收的邻居信息更新。机器人对无需等待所有机器人完成通信轮次即可更新，所选状态消息的共识坐标在固定状态表示下与团队规模无关。将AC-DC应用于动态优先级多机器人遍历搜索：一个共识流估计团队访问以实现运动协调，另一个融合区域测量信息以更新不确定性地图和搜索目标。

Result: 在多达80个机器人、12种设置、每种20对试验中，AC-DC在去中心化方法中具有最低的平均归一化协方差迹AUC和尝试建模通信载荷。平均而言，相比最先进基线，AC-DC的AUC降低27.5%，通信流量减少8.7倍。在固定600×600 m扩展测试中，120个机器人时AC-DC使用19.3 MB，接近理想集中式基线的19.2 MB，同时保持点对点通信。

Conclusion: AC-DC在有限通信条件下实现了可扩展、低通信量且高效的动态平均一致性，并在多机器人遍历搜索中优于现有去中心化方法，通信开销接近理想集中式基线。

Abstract: We study scalable peer-to-peer dynamic average consensus (DC) for multi-robot systems under finite-range, finite-rate, and interference-constrained communication. We introduce Adaptive Communication for Dynamic Average Consensus (AC-DC), which jointly adapts Who communicates with whom, When, and over What parts of the consensus state, using local inputs and successfully received neighbor information. Each robot's consensus state estimates the current average of the robots' local inputs. AC-DC updates these estimates as local inputs change and averages the values exchanged between robot pairs. In AC-DC, robot pairs update without waiting for every robot to complete a communication round, and the selected-state messages carry consensus state coordinates independent of team size for a fixed state representation. We apply AC-DC to dynamic-priority multi-robot ergodic search: one consensus stream estimates team visitation for motion coordination, while the other fuses regional measurement information to update uncertainty maps and search targets. Across twelve settings with up to 80 robots and 20 paired trials per setting, AC-DC has the lowest mean (i) normalized covariance-trace area under the curve (AUC) and (ii) attempted modeled communication payload among the compared decentralized methods. Averaged across settings, AC-DC achieves paired AUC reductions of 27.5% relative to state-of-the-art baselines, with 8.7x less communication traffic. As the number of robots increases, we observe that AC-DC's communication payload approaches that of the ideal centralized baseline (one ground compute-station communicating directly with all robots): with 120 robots in a fixed 600 x 600 m scaling test, AC-DC uses 19.3 MB versus 19.2 MB for the ideal centralized baseline, while remaining peer-to-peer.

</details>


### [164] [SPARSER: Sparse Variable Projection by Exploiting Separable Structure in Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24708)
*Nikolas R. Sanderson,Andrew Fishberg,Haoyu Han,Heng Yang,Jonathan P. How,Hanumant Singh,Michael Everett,Alan Papalia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPARSER，一个联合利用可分离性与稀疏性的VarPro框架，用于规范对称的机器人感知问题，在SLAM/SNL/SfM基准上较SOTA提速5–7倍，鲁棒变体较GNC提速2–16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知需解大规模非线性最小二乘（NLS）。稀疏性已被广泛利用，而"可分离性"（如视觉路标在残差中线性出现，固定位姿后可解析求解）被利用不足。VarPro可解析消去线性变量获得良好性质的降维问题，但因规范对称（如全局平移旋转不变性）而难以直接应用于机器人感知。

Method: SPARSER：针对规范对称问题的VarPro框架，联合利用可分离性与稀疏性。构建无矩阵的Schur补算子以高效评估降维代价、梯度与Hessian向量积，从而可接入迭代NLS求解器；刻画适用问题类并识别可进一步解析简化的常见情形，且证明基于IRLS的鲁棒代价能保留大部分可利用结构。

Result: 在合成与真实SLAM、SNL、SfM基准上，SPARSER在CPU与GPU上平均比SOTA基线快5–7倍，个别数据集上加速超40倍。在含外点的多机器人SLAM数据上，鲁棒变体比SOTA的GNC求解器快2–16倍。已开源C++代码及全部数据集。

Conclusion: SPARSER通过解析消去线性变量并保持稀疏性，有效解决了规范对称下VarPro的应用难题，显著提升机器人感知中大规模NLS求解效率，并开源代码与数据。

Abstract: Robotic perception often requires solving large nonlinear least-squares (NLS) problems. While sparsity has been widely exploited to scale solvers, a complementary and underused structure is \emph{separability}: some variables, such as visual landmarks, appear linearly in the residuals and admit a closed-form solution once the remaining variables, such as poses, are fixed. Variable projection (VarPro) exploits this structure by analytically eliminating the linear variables, yielding a reduced problem with favorable computational properties. However, its use in robotic perception has been limited by gauge symmetries, such as invariance to global translations and rotations, which introduce challenges for standard VarPro methods.
  We present SPARSER (\textbf{S}parsity \textbf{P}reserving \textbf{A}nalytic \textbf{R}eduction for \textbf{S}eparable \textbf{R}obotic \textbf{P}erception), a VarPro framework for gauge-symmetric problems that jointly exploits separability and sparsity. Our method constructs a \emph{matrix-free Schur complement operator} for efficient evaluation of reduced costs, gradients, and Hessian-vector products, enabling integration with iterative NLS solvers. We characterize the applicable problem class, identify common cases admitting further analytical simplifications, and show that IRLS-based robust costs preserve most of the exploitable structure.
  Across synthetic and real SLAM, SNL, and SfM benchmarks, SPARSER is on average $5\times$--$7\times$ faster than state-of-the-art baselines on CPU and GPU, with gains exceeding $40\times$ on individual datasets. On outlier-corrupted multi-robot SLAM data, the robust variant is $2\times$--$16\times$ faster than a state-of-the-art GNC solver. We release open-source C++ code and all datasets.

</details>


### [165] [LLM-based Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for MyBuddy Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24742)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并在13轴MyBuddy人形机器人（Raspberry Pi驱动）上实现了一个基于大语言模型的多功能对话式AI知识助手，融合语音识别、互联网知识检索、对话管理与语音合成，实现智能多轮对话与情感支持型人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在以人为本的应用中日益普及，但其智能对话与自然的知识辅助能力受限于传统基于规则的对话系统、预定义回答以及有限的知识库，难以满足自然、自适应、上下文感知的人机交互需求。

Method: 以LLM作为核心，驱动语言理解与AI推理，并结合实时语音识别、通过可扩展互联网引擎（如Wikipedia、arXiv）进行知识检索、灵活的对话管理以及自然语音合成，集成部署于Raspberry Pi驱动的13轴MyBuddy人形机器人上。

Result: 系统实现了更智能的多轮连续对话能力与高级情感支持型人机交互，突破了传统规则系统在知识范围与交互自然度上的限制。

Conclusion: LLM为自然、自适应、上下文感知的人机交互提供了强大基础，所提出的LLM对话式AI知识助手可有效提升人形机器人的对话智能与知识辅助能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly being popular and developed for human-centered applications, yet their ability to provide intelligent conversations and natural interactive knowledge assistance remains constrained by traditional rule-based dialogue systems, pre-defined responses and limited knowledge repositories. Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful foundation for enabling natural, adaptive, and context-aware Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), which provides a significant opportunity to address such limitations by enabling robots to understand natural speech language, reason over complicated queries, maintain high-quality conversational context, and generate knowledge-rich responses. In this work, we originally present and implement an LLM-based versatile Conversational AI Knowledge Assistant for the Raspberry-Pi-powered 13-Axis MyBuddy humanoid robot, which integrates LLM-driven language understanding and AI reasoning with real-time speech recognition, knowledge retrieval via extensible access of internet engines (e.g., Wikipedia, arXiv), flexible dialogue management, and natural speech synthesis to enable much more intelligent multi-turn continuous conversations and advanced emotional-support Human-Robot Interaction.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Visual Quality: A Study of Test-Time Planning with World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24745)
*Jianhao Yuan,Yu Yuan,Benjamin Ramtoula,Lukas Vierling,Paul Newman,Lars Kunze,Philip Torr,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证考察世界动作模型(WAM)利用想象未来进行动作选择规划的潜力，发现虽然存在可观的选择上界，但现有基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展等评分选择器难以有效恢复这一增益，说明"产生"有后果的动作选择与"识别"它并不等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型能同时生成动作及其视觉后果预测，理论上可通过采样多个动作、比较想象结果、选择最优预测来规划。然而如何利用想象未来指导动作选择尚不清晰，作者希望检验这一规划潜力是否真能被现有方法兑现。

Method: 在相同状态控制分析中，先估计oracle选择上界（选出真实结果最佳的候选）；再测试基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展的选择器作为控制干预。进一步通过反事实分支分析动作是否导致不同结果、差异是否在预测中可见、评分是否识别这些差异，并跨轨迹阶段进行完整动作执行评估，比较各评分选择与学习价值函数的增益。

Result: 同状态控制分析中，最优选择将成功率从均匀随机选择的68.9%提升至79.2%（oracle上界）。基于视觉质量、物理一致性、任务进展的测试选择器虽能带来更高成功率，但增益不均衡，匹配的选择器远未恢复测得的可用提升。反事实分支显示选择机会集中在初始候选集少数决策中，动作分散度与结果覆盖面并不必然同增。跨阶段完整执行评估中，测试评分同样仅恢复少量可用提升，仅学习价值带来微小增益。

Conclusion: 产生有后果的动作选择与在生成未来中识别它之间存在本质差距。应通过WAM预测对决策的有用性来评估，而非仅看视觉质量。

Abstract: World action models generate actions together with visual predictions of their consequences. These paired outputs create the potential for planning by sampling multiple actions from one state, comparing their imagined outcomes, and choosing the action with the most promising predicted outcome. However, how to use imagined futures to guide action selection remains unclear. We examine this planning potential empirically. First, we estimate an oracle upper bound on selection by choosing the sampled candidate whose realised outcome is best. In a controlled same-state analysis, this choice raises success from 68.9% under uniform random selection to 79.2%. We then test selectors based on visual quality, physical consistency, and task progression as controlled interventions. Some tested selectors yield higher observed success, but the gains are uneven and the matched selectors leave much of the measured opportunity unrecovered. To investigate this gap, we examine whether sampled actions lead to different outcomes, whether these differences are visible in the predictions, and whether a score recognises them. Counterfactual branching from the same states shows that selection opportunity is concentrated in relatively few decisions in the initial candidate sets. Action spread and outcome coverage need not increase together. In a further evaluation across trajectory phases with complete action execution, the tested scores again recover little of the available improvement despite a small gain from learned value. These findings distinguish producing consequential action choices from recognising them in generated futures, motivating the evaluation of WAM predictions through their usefulness for decisions rather than visual quality alone.

</details>


### [167] [D-JEPA: A Decision-Aligned Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24749)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chengyu Wu,Qifu Wen,Feiyang You,Chenglong Zhang,Shuyang Hao,Xi Lin,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 D-JEPA，一种决策对齐的潜在世界模型，通过从执行结果中学习候选未来之间的决策相关关系，使潜在距离更好地反映动作能否成功，从而改进动作选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型虽能预测动作后果，但预测准确并不保证潜在距离能反映哪个候选动作会执行成功。作者发现"决策局部预测差距"：在少数竞争执行的未来中，被预测更接近目标的候选反而可能产生比可选方案更差的真实结果。

Method: 提出 D-JEPA，学习候选未来之间与决策相关的关系。采用一个有界、置换等变算子，对相对目标的预测特征与序数证据进行联合推理，在最关键的决策处细化预训练预测几何；通过受限预测器适配与共享序数接口将这种对齐扩展到互补的预测几何；并在 JEPA 兼容的未来表示中实现所学决策结构，支持原生潜在距离规划。

Result: 在潜在控制、操作、预训练动作生成模型、实体机器人和自动驾驶等任务上均提升了动作选择表现：PushT 成功率达 87.89%，RoboTwin 平均提升 15.04 个点，实体机器人任务提升 17 个点。

Conclusion: 决策相关的关系结构可作为连接预测式世界建模与有效控制的直接桥梁。

Abstract: Latent world models predict the consequences of actions, but accurate prediction does not guarantee that latent distance reflects which candidate will execute successfully. We identify a decision-local prediction gap: among the few futures competing for execution, a candidate predicted closer to the goal can produce a worse realized outcome than an available alternative. We introduce D-JEPA, a decision-aligned latent world model that learns decision-relevant relations among candidate futures from executed outcomes. A bounded, permutation-equivariant operator jointly reasons over goal-relative predictive features and ordinal evidence, refining pretrained predictive geometry where action choices are most consequential. Restricted predictor adaptation and a shared ordinal interface extend this alignment across complementary predictive geometries. D-JEPA further realizes the learned decision structure in JEPA-compatible future representations, enabling deployment through native latent-distance planning. Evaluations across latent control, manipulation, pretrained action-producing models, physical robots and autonomous driving demonstrate improved action selection, including 87.89% success on PushT, a 15.04-point average gain on RoboTwin, and a 17-point gain on physical robot tasks. These results establish decision-relevant relational structure as a direct bridge between predictive world modeling and effective control.

</details>


### [168] [A Switched Adaptive Control Framework for Aerial Manipulators Under Dynamic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24761)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Saksham Gupta,Amitabh Sharma,Sarthak Mishra,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy,Simone Baldi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于切换的自适应控制框架，用于空中机械臂，无需先验知识即可应对系统动力学突变与状态相关不确定性，并通过对比实验验证其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在执行任务时会发生动态过渡，系统动力学出现突变或不连续，现有空中操作方法并非针对切换工况设计；同时难以处理飞行器与机械臂之间的强耦合以及由此产生的状态相关不确定性。因此需要一种能保证过渡阶段稳定性的控制方法。

Method: 提出基于切换的自适应控制框架。该框架不依赖飞行器-机械臂耦合及状态相关不确定性的先验知识，并提供一类切换信号，用以刻画系统可保证稳定的过渡阶段。

Result: 通过对比实验验证了所提基于切换的框架相对于现有先进方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于切换的自适应控制框架能够在不依赖先验知识的情况下保证空中机械臂在动态过渡中的稳定操作，且实验证明其性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: Aerial manipulators represent the forefront of aerial robotics. Although potentially capable of complex interaction tasks, controlling aerial manipulators throughout the dynamic transitions occurring during task execution presents significant challenges. Abrupt or discontinuous changes in system dynamics generated by the transitions suggest the use of a switched approach, yet the available aerial manipulation methods are not designed for coping with switched regimes. In addition, most available methods fall short in coping with the tight couplings between the aerial vehicle and the manipulator, as well as in coping with the state-dependent uncertainties arising from the difficulty in modeling such couplings. We propose a switched-based adaptive control framework for aerial manipulators not relying on a priori knowledge of the vehicle-manipulator couplings and of state-dependent uncertainties. To guarantee stable manipulation despite changes in system dynamics, the framework provides a class of switching signals characterizing those transition phases for which the system is guaranteed to remain stable. Comparative experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed switched-based framework over the state of the art.

</details>


### [169] [H2RBench: A Real-to-Sim Benchmark for Evaluating Human-to-Robot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24778)
*Chuyang Xiao,Haotian Zhan,Sriram Krishna,Peilin Meng,Muhammad Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出H2RBench，一个基于Real2Sim的基准，用于标准化评估人视频到机器人（H2R）迁移方法，并验证仿真性能可较好预测真实机器人性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频演示中学习机器人操作策略具有可扩展性潜力，但现有H2R迁移方法在任务套件、场景布局、物体实例和机器人监督量等设置上各不相同，导致难以公平比较。

Method: H2RBench基于真实人类视频演示与仿真机器人演示构建标准化评估协议，涵盖四个具有不同交互需求的操作任务，并评估多种采用不同策略弥合具身差距的代表性H2R迁移方法。

Result: 系统刻画了各方法随人类演示数量增加的性能缩放规律，发现不同方法利用额外人类数据的能力差异显著；仿真性能与真实机器人性能总体高度相关（Pearson r=0.89，Spearman ρ=0.85，MMRV=0.06）。

Conclusion: H2RBench是一个实用且可扩展的基准，可在真实部署前用于H2R方法的对比评估。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies from human video demonstrations constitutes a promising avenue for scalable robot learning. However, comparing different human-to-robot (H2R) transfer methods remains challenging, as existing approaches are evaluated under different settings, including differing task suites, scene layouts, object instances, and amounts of robot supervision. To address this challenge, we present H2RBench, a Real2Sim benchmark for evaluating H2R transfer methods. H2RBench provides a standardized protocol built on real human video demonstrations and simulated robot demonstrations, and includes four manipulation tasks spanning diverse interaction requirements. We evaluate multiple representative H2R transfer methods, each adopting a different strategy for bridging the embodiment gap. Using H2RBench, we systematically characterize how each method scales with the amount of human demonstrations, revealing that methods differ substantially in their ability to leverage additional human data. We further show that simulation performance is broadly predictive of real-world robot performance, with an overall Pearson correlation of r = 0.89, Spearman correlation of \r{ho} = 0.85 and Mean Maximum Rank Violation (MMRV) of 0.06 across method-task configurations. These results establish H2RBench as a practical and scalable benchmark for comparative H2R evaluation prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [170] [Uranus: Building the Next-Generation Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24815)
*Wenkang Qin,Yukun Zhou,Noah Shen,Jisong Cai,Dongxiao Mao,Baicheng Li,Yue Zhang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Uranus，一个基于联合轨迹条件自回归扩散模型的数据驱动机器人模拟器，支持流式开放式长时序生成、低延迟生成和可扩展的机器人控制，并通过定量与定性评估验证其能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的仿真是机器人数据生成、策略训练、评估与安全迭代的关键，但真实世界交互成本高昂，而传统模拟器构建需要大量人工。因此需要一种数据驱动的、可扩展的机器人模拟器。

Method: 提出 Uranus，围绕联合轨迹条件（joint-trajectory-conditioned）的自回归扩散模型构建。在线接收未来关节位置轨迹，逐步自回归生成一个潜帧（对应四帧 RGB），无固定时间跨度；并通过推理优化实现低延迟。

Result: Uranus 具备三项能力：(1) 流式开放式 rollout，无固定 horizon，每步生成对应四帧 RGB 的潜帧；(2) 推理优化后达到 24 FPS 低延迟；(3) 可扩展、可拓展的机器人控制，为多视角同步生成提供统一接口，适应不同机器人形态与相机配置。在分布内与分布外数据上进行了全面的定量与定性评估。

Conclusion: Uranus 是一个数据驱动的机器人模拟器，能客观评估其能力并明确指出当前局限。作者开源代码与模型权重，以促进社区使用。

Abstract: Scalable simulation is essential for robot data generation, policy training, evaluation, and safe iteration, yet real-world interaction is costly and conventional simulators require labor-intensive construction. We present Uranus, a data-driven robot simulator built around a joint-trajectory-conditioned autoregressive diffusion model. Uranus offers three key capabilities: (1) streaming, open-ended rollout, which receives future joint-position trajectories online and autoregressively generates one latent frame per step, corresponding to four RGB frames, without a fixed horizon; (2) low-latency generation, achieving 24 FPS after inference optimization; and (3) scalable, extensible robot control, providing a unified interface for synchronized multi-view generation across diverse robot embodiments and camera configurations. We conduct comprehensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing an objective assessment of Uranus and clearly identifying its current limitations. We release the code and model weights to empower the community with practical tools and insights.

</details>


### [171] [Minimum Time Trajectories for a Car-Like Mobile Robot Moving with Rigid Wheels Under Non-Sliding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24832)
*Joseph Ben-Asher,Elon Rimon,Leeor Ravina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无遮挡环境下类车移动机器人最小时间轨迹，在动力学层面引入非滑动约束，扩展运动学时间最优路径基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有类车机器人时间最优轨迹研究基于运动学模型，假设车轮纯滚动、忽略前后轮非滑动约束，该假设只能通过机器人动力学处理，因此需要将非滑动约束纳入时间最优轨迹求解。

Method: 基于机器人动力学建立非滑动约束，将其与运动学模型时间最优路径基元结合，新增三种与非滑动约束相关的时间最优路径基元，与原有十二种运动学基元共同构成完整时间最优轨迹，并给出非滑动基元的近似解析解。

Result: 得到由十二种运动学基元和三种非滑动基元组成的类车机器人时间最优轨迹集合，并提供非滑动路径基元的近似解析解；通过代表性机动的算例展示非滑动约束对时间最优轨迹的影响。

Conclusion: 非滑动约束会显著影响类车机器人时间最优轨迹，将动力学非滑动约束引入路径基元可更准确地描述实际时间最优机动。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum time trajectoriesvof a car-like mobile robot navigating in an obstacle free environment. The robot, with forward and backward speeds, is controlled by bounded front-wheels acceleration and limited front-wheels steering rate. The paper extends previous results which solved this problem for the kinematic car-like robot. However, the kinematic model assumes pure rolling at the wheels ground contacts. This assumption requires non-sliding constraints for the front and rear wheels that can only be handled by the robot dynamics. This paper formulates the non-sliding constraints based on the robot dynamics then augments the kinematic model time-optimal path primitives with three new path primitives associated with the non-sliding constraints. The three non-sliding path primitives together with the kinematic model twelve path primitives form the car-like robot time optimal trajectories. Approximate analytic solutions for the non-sliding path primitives are also provided. Examples study the time-optimal path primitives along representative maneuvers, illustrating how the non-sliding constraints influence the time optimal trajectories of the car-like robot.

</details>


### [172] [PredActor: Predictive Action Diffusion for Steerable Onboard Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24840)
*Lei Ye,Haibo Gao,Yitang Li,Peng Xu,Zetong Jing,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Ziqi Han,Xue Wang,Jianhua Sun,Cewu Lu,Hao Zhao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredActor：预测式动作扩散策略，仅用本体感受观测，联合生成可执行动作与内部未来状态轨迹，实现文本条件行为、测试时引导与扰动响应，并可在Jetson Orin NX实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能灵活生成运动，但用于反馈响应式人形控制仍困难：分层系统受限于参考轨迹超出追踪器能力；纯动作扩散缺乏显式未来状态轨迹，难以支持测试时运动目标；联合状态-动作扩散控制器多依赖特权全身状态，且行为选择与测试时引导能力碎片化。因此需要一种无需特权状态、可直接执行的统一引导策略。

Method: 提出PredActor，一种预测式动作扩散策略。以本体感受历史与可选任务上下文为条件，联合生成可执行动作和内部未来状态轨迹；采用无分类器引导增强文本条件行为，分类器引导将预测状态导向测试时目标；仅执行动作，无需独立运动参考追踪器，也不使用外部估计的全身状态作为策略输入。为便于机载部署，采用滚动去噪与计算保持型运行时优化。

Result: 仿真中达到全部15个目标位置，文本检索得分0.580，高于条件动作扩散的0.373，扰动存活率相近；在Jetson Orin NX上完整回调中位数16.790 ms、p95为19.383 ms，均低于20 ms控制周期。已在Unitree G1上部署，仿真与硬件评估展示文本条件运动、扰动响应、手柄控制与语义插值。

Conclusion: PredActor将互补的引导能力整合进单一直接执行策略，仅凭本体感受观测即可实现文本条件、测试时目标引导与扰动响应，并在真实人形机器人上满足实时控制要求。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer flexible motion generation, but translating this flexibility into feedback-responsive humanoid control remains challenging. Hierarchical systems steer motion through references that may exceed a separate tracker's capabilities, leaving recovery and physical execution largely to the tracker. Action-only diffusion generates actions directly but lacks an explicit future-state trajectory for test-time motion objectives. Joint state-action diffusion provides this representation, yet representative controllers often depend on privileged full-body states, and support for learned behavior selection and test-time motion steering remains fragmented. We present PredActor, a predictive action diffusion policy that brings these complementary steering capabilities into one directly executed policy using proprioceptive observations. Conditioned on proprioceptive history and optional task context, PredActor jointly generates executable actions and an internal future-state trajectory. Classifier-free guidance strengthens text-conditioned behavior, while classifier guidance steers predicted states toward test-time objectives. Only actions are executed, without a separate motion-reference tracker or externally estimated full-body states as policy inputs. In simulation, PredActor reaches all 15 destination targets and achieves a text retrieval score of 0.580, compared with 0.373 for conditional action diffusion, with similar observed disturbance survival. To make this guided policy practical onboard, rolling denoising and computation-preserving runtime optimizations reduce the complete callback to 16.790 ms median and 19.383 ms p95 on a Jetson Orin NX, both below the 20 ms control period. We deploy PredActor on a Unitree G1; evaluations across simulation and physical hardware demonstrate text-conditioned motion, disturbance response, joystick control, and semantic interpolation.

</details>


### [173] [CAST: Collision-Aware Assembly with Construction Robots using Simultaneous Trajectory Estimation and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24841)
*Karthik Shaji,Chisung Kim,John D'Amato,Edvard Bruun,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于单一因子图的轨迹估计与规划框架，用于多机器人建筑施工，可同时处理碰撞约束与学到的缆绳约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在建筑领域应用前景广阔，但施工环境具有高维构型空间和大量碰撞避免约束（其他机器人、装配物体、工作空间边界），同时重型机器人系统存在刚度与振动引起的不确定性，传统方法难以应对。因此需要一个能适应工作空间变化、支持同步高维运动规划的统一框架。

Method: 采用单一因子图统一进行轨迹估计与规划，将测得的机器人状态与显式碰撞约束及学习得到的缆绳约束共同纳入模型。该框架支持变化的工作空间，并能在考虑重型机器人系统振动导致的不确定性情况下，实现同步的高维机器人运动规划。

Result: 在建造梁柱（post-and-lintel）结构的任务中验证了框架的有效性：一台机械臂作为木材抓取器，另一台机器人作为钉钉紧固器，两者协同完成施工。

Conclusion: 该单一因子图框架能够成功实现多机器人协作施工，兼顾碰撞避免、学习约束与重型系统的不确定性，证明了其在复杂高维建筑任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems have shown increasing viability in construction due to their ability to execute high-precision actions while reducing human exposure to hazardous tasks. However, these environments have high-dimensional configuration spaces and possess substantial collision-avoidance constraints, which include other robots, assembly objects, and workspace boundaries. We utilize a single factor graph for trajectory estimation and planning that incorporates measured robot states together with explicit collision and learned cable constraints. This supports changing workspaces and enables synchronized, high-dimensional robot motion planning while accounting for the stiff, vibration-induced uncertainty of heavy robotic systems. We demonstrate the success of our framework on the construction of a post-and-lintel structure using one robot arm as a timber gripper, and a second robot as a nail-fastener.

</details>


### [174] [Range-Aided SLAM Initialization Exploiting Accurate Heading Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24846)
*Isabel Lougheed,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于测距辅助同时定位与建图（RA-SLAM）的新型初始化方法，利用AUV高精度航向信息，通过GTRS与线性最小二乘两步求解应答器与机器人位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用SLAM问题具有可分离结构，在已知机器人航向时可线性求解地标与机器人位置。AUV导航中通常可获得高精度航向信息，因此可利用该特性解决测距应答器与机器人位置初始化问题。

Method: 方法分两步：第一步，基于广义信赖域子问题（GTRS），在给定非线性测距量测下求解应答器相对于AUV的位置；第二步，利用应答器相对位置与AUV已知航向，通过求解线性最小二乘问题估计应答器和AUV位置。

Result: 该方法在真实AUV数据集上进行了验证，数据集中包含长基线（LBL）测距量测以及由惯性导航系统（INS）提供的高精度航向信息，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所得到的应答器与AUV位置估计，结合高精度航向信息，为通用非线性RA-SLAM问题提供了可靠的初始化方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel initialization method for range-aided simultaneous localization and mapping (RA-SLAM). The general SLAM problem has a well-known separable structure where landmark and robot positions can be solved for in a linear fashion given known robot headings. This paper considers the case where highly accurate heading information is available, which is typical of autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) navigation, to solve for the range transponder and robot positions. The proposed approach consists of two steps. First, using a generalized trust region subproblem (GTRS), the positions of the transponders relative to the AUV are solved for given the nonlinear range measurements. Second, the relative transponder positions and the known heading of the AUV are used to estimate the transponder and AUV positions by solving a linear least-squares problem. These transponder and AUV position estimates, combined with the highly accurate heading information, provide a reliable initialization method for the general nonlinear RA-SLAM problem. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is tested on a real-world AUV dataset where long baseline (LBL) range measurements are provided in concert with highly accurate heading information provided by an inertial navigation system (INS).

</details>


### [175] [SE(3) Neural Potential Fields for 6-DoF Trajectory Planning Directly from Images Without Explicit 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24864)
*Jeffrey Eiyike,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于SE(3)神经网络势场的方法，用导航函数（自由空间测地距离）监督，从RGB图像直接学习无碰撞抓取轨迹，避免碰撞与停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统6-DoF抓取需重建3D场景并规划，代价是精度和算力；从图像学习的势场又继承了人工势场的缺陷：吸引与排斥梯度抵消时路径会擦过障碍而非绕开，甚至中途停滞。

Method: 从带位姿的RGB图像学习SE(3)神经势场，并以导航函数作为监督信号——即训练时从同一批图像恢复的自由空间中到抓取点的测地距离，从而消除上述两类失败。

Result: 在两个桌面场景、UR10从被障碍阻挡的起点执行：势场从每个起点都能收敛到抓取点3cm内，所有路径相对真值几何均无碰撞（仅图像监督时为25%和0%）；平均间隙从不足1cm升至8.6-8.8cm；臂杆接触从20.6-50.4%降至2.7-5.5%。执行抓取成功率90.0%和40.0%，剩余失败源于笛卡尔执行器拒动。规划约2s，RRT*在重建上需67-133s；在同一离线测试框架下二者相当，部署差距源于稠密重建的碰撞检测成本。

Conclusion: 以导航函数监督的SE(3)神经势场可从图像直接生成无碰撞抓取轨迹，显著优于纯图像监督，并在部署效率上大幅优于基于重建的RRT*，差距来自碰撞检测成本而非规划器复杂度。

Abstract: Reaching a 6-DoF grasp pose in clutter requires a collision-free trajectory, conventionally obtained by reconstructing the scene in 3D and planning inside that reconstruction, at the cost of its accuracy and compute. Potential fields learned directly from images remove that dependency but inherit the classical weakness of artificial potential fields: where attractive and repulsive gradients cancel, the descent grazes the obstacle instead of going around it, and can stall short of the goal. We present an SE(3) neural potential field learned from posed RGB images and supervised with a navigation function, the geodesic distance to the grasp through free space recovered from those same images during training, which removes both failures. On two tabletop scenes, from obstacle-blocked starts executed on a UR10, the field converges within 3 cm of the grasp from every start and every path it executes is collision-free against the ground-truth geometry, against 25% and 0% under image supervision alone; mean clearance rises from under a centimeter to 8.6-8.8 cm and arm-link contacts fall from 20.6-50.4% to 2.7-5.5% of executed configurations. Executed grasp success is 90.0% and 40.0% on the two scenes, the residual failures being refusals of the Cartesian executor rather than of the field. Planning takes about 2 s against 67-133 s for RRT* on a reconstruction of the same images, though under a common offline harness the two are comparable: the deployed margin is the cost of collision-checking a dense reconstruction, not planner complexity.

</details>


### [176] [DualWAM: Dual-System World Action Models for Asynchronous Global Planning and Local Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24868)
*Yixin Zheng,Jiangran Lyu,Yuntian Deng,Kai Liu,Yizhou Zhou,Yizhou Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao,He Wang,Zhizheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双系统世界动作模型，解耦全局规划与局部精修，实现高频闭环控制并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型通过生成长动作块分摊推理成本，牺牲了闭环响应能力。

Method: 采用双系统架构：系统二周期性对更广域的世界-动作块进行高噪声双向去噪以建立全局规划；系统一从中间去噪状态提取时间对齐的短窗口，结合最新腕部观测完成低噪声精修；两者沿共享去噪轨迹异步运行。

Result: 在Franka和Galbot零样本操作任务上，平均成功率较最强基线提升4.5个百分点，关键路径加速16.6倍；角色匹配的自我中心与UMI数据提升成功率14个百分点，且支持边缘-云部署并显著降低通信开销。
